Extrinsically, skills derived from TMI's task models improve held-out task accuracy by 30.0% over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [114] [G-CARL: Grounded Checklist-Aligned Reward Learning for Patient-Oriented Medical Report Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20331)
*Shiao Xie,Siyu Chen,Jianwei Lv,Bo Yuan,Yujin Wang,Xiandong Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出患者导向的医学报告解读（PMRI）新任务，并设计了G-CARL框架——一种基于检索验证和检查清单对齐的强化学习框架，在构建的MMedReport基准上优于现有基线，实现医学事实准确性与患者沟通质量的双重优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者对医学报告的个性化解读需求日益增长，需要同时满足医学事实性和上下文相关的患者沟通，但现有医学视觉语言任务未能充分捕捉这两个要求。这两个目标在可验证性上根本不同却又紧密耦合，传统监督微调和整体强化学习范式难以联合优化。

Method: 提出PMRI任务（基于用户查询和对话历史生成准确且通俗的医学报告解读）。提出G-CARL框架：1）多源检索用于原子声明验证（grounded）；2）上下文感知、实例特定的加权检查清单用于响应覆盖（checklist-aligned）；3）为事实性、用户需求满足和表达质量提供结构化监督，同时不限制响应多样性。构建MMedReport真实世界基准及临床医生设计的三维评估协议。

Result: 大量实验表明G-CARL在整体质量、声明级精确度和检查清单召回率上持续优于现有后训练基线。临床医生的配对偏好评估进一步确认G-CARL生成的解读更准确且更符合患者需求。

Conclusion: G-CARL展示了将接地验证与检查清单对齐相结合的有效性，为医学报告个性化解读这类"可验证性不同但耦合紧密"的多目标优化问题提供了新的解决范式。

Abstract: Personalized interpretation of medical reports has emerged as an increasingly important need among patients. Addressing this need requires both evidence-grounded medical factuality and context-dependent patient communication, yet existing medical vision-language tasks do not adequately capture these dual requirements. To bridge this gap, we introduce Patient-oriented Medical Report Interpretation (PMRI), a novel open-ended multimodal generation task that requires models to explain medical reports in accurate and accessible language based on a user's query and dialogue history. These two objectives differ fundamentally in their verifiability, yet remain tightly coupled, making them difficult to optimize jointly under conventional supervised fine-tuning and holistic reinforcement learning paradigms. To address this challenge, we propose G-CARL, a grounded, checklist-aligned reinforcement learning framework that combines multi-source retrieval for atomic claim verification with context-aware, instance-specific weighted checklists for response coverage, providing structured supervision for factuality, user-demand satisfaction, and expression quality without constraining response diversity. We further construct MMedReport, a real-world PMRI benchmark, along with a clinician-designed three-dimensional evaluation protocol. Extensive experiments demonstrate that G-CARL consistently outperforms existing post-training baselines in overall quality, claim-level precision, and checklist recall. Pairwise preference evaluation by clinicians further confirms that G-CARL produces interpretations that are more accurate and better aligned with patient needs.

</details>


### [115] [ConceptGuard: Benchmarking Context-Sensitive Unlearning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20338)
*Sahil Kale,Ian Harris*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出概念级机器遗忘（unlearning）的新评估框架ConceptGuard，基于"双用途概念"（可同时用于有害和良性情境的概念）构建基准，揭示现有遗忘方法在概念层面表现不佳，无法有效实现上下文区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM遗忘方法和基准依赖于不相关事实的分离遗忘集与保留集，仅评估简单直接的事实回忆，无法捕捉遗忘的关键要求——在消除有害行为的同时保留良性相关知识。有效遗忘必须在概念层面运作。

Method: 引入"双用途概念"概念，构建ConceptGuard基准：遗忘集和保留集在概念使用上显式互补；评估是意图敏感的（intent-sensitive），以最大化上下文分离为目标；采用ROUGE和概念级指标进行评估。

Result: 现有遗忘技术在此设置下表现不佳：上下文分离能力弱，ROUGE和概念级指标表现差；存在强遗忘-效用权衡、上下文敏感度提升有限、概念级控制一致性差等问题。

Conclusion: 概念级遗忘是更符合现实安全需求的方向，现有方法无法满足此要求，需要开发与真实世界安全需求更对齐的遗忘方法。数据集已公开。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly require selective removal of harmful or sensitive knowledge, called unlearning, yet existing methods and benchmarks fail to evaluate this capability completely. Current approaches rely on disjoint forget and retain sets composed of independent facts, and measure success using simple and direct factual recall. This framing fails to capture a key requirement of unlearning, namely the ability to eliminate harmful behaviors while preserving benign and beneficial knowledge. We argue that effective unlearning must operate at the level of concepts, ensuring complete removal of unsafe applications while maintaining their correct and useful usage, thereby achieving conceptually meaningful and complete unlearning. To better evaluate unlearning techniques from such a practical viewpoint, we introduce the notion of dual-use concepts: concepts that can be used in both harmful and benign contexts. Building on these concepts, we construct a benchmark called ConceptGuard where forget and retain sets are explicitly complementary in concept usage. Our benchmark uniquely enables unlearning to be explored and gauged at the level of concepts, instead of sparse facts, and evaluation is intent-sensitive with the goal of maximizing contextual separation to promote safer behavior. We demonstrate that current unlearning techniques perform poorly under this setting, showing weak contextual separation alongside poor performance in ROUGE and concept-level metrics. Our results reveal strong forgetting-utility trade-offs, limited gains in contextual sensitivity, and poor consistency in concept-level control across methods, and provide ideas for unlearning approaches that better align with real-world safety requirements. Our dataset is publicly available.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [Product Gap Mechanisms for Multi-Facility Location](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19633)
*Jianhao Jia*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究实轴上多设施部署的随机策略证明机制，提出"Product-Gap"机制：以与连续间隙乘积成比例的概率选择k个上报位置并在此开设设施。对于任意k≥2，该机制对社交成本达到紧的2k近似比；对k=2和k=3具有期望策略证明性，但对k≥4不成立。此外，针对两个设施，将Product-Gap与Lu等人的Proportional机制混合，得到一个优化且与报告无关的混合机制，紧近似比为(74+4√3)/23≈3.519，优于此前4的因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多设施选址问题中，设计兼具策略证明性与良好近似比的机制一直是个挑战。此前针对两个设施的机制近似比仅为4，有改进空间；同时需要系统性研究k个设施的通用机制，并在策略证明性与近似保证之间寻找更优平衡。

Method: 提出Product-Gap机制：以与所选k个位置间连续间隙乘积成比例的概率分布选择k个上报位置并开设设施。分析其社交成本近似比为2k（紧界）；证明k=2和k=3时具有期望策略证明性，k≥4时不成立。针对两个设施，将Product-Gap与Proportional机制进行优化混合（报告无关的凸组合），推导出紧近似比(74+4√3)/23。

Result: Product-Gap机制对任意k≥2实现紧的2k社交成本近似比；对k=2和k=3分别给出策略证明的4-和6-近似机制；对k≥4失去策略证明性。混合机制对两个设施实现策略证明且紧近似比为约3.519，优于此前最优的4。

Conclusion: 该工作系统地推进了多设施随机策略证明机制的设计边界，Product-Gap提供了统一框架，而混合策略进一步缩小了理论下界与可达上界之间的差距，为后续研究提供了新的思路与基准。

Abstract: We study randomized strategyproof mechanisms for locating multiple facilities on the real line. We introduce the \emph{Product-Gap mechanism}, which selects $k$ reported locations with probability proportional to the product of the consecutive gaps between them and opens facilities at the selected locations. We prove that, for every $k\geq 2$, the mechanism achieves a tight approximation ratio of $2k$ for social cost. We then study its incentive properties and show that it is strategyproof in expectation for $k=2$ and $k=3$, but is not strategyproof for $k\geq 4$. In particular, the mechanism gives strategyproof $4$- and $6$-approximations for two and three facilities, respectively. Finally, for two facilities, we combine Product-Gap with the Proportional mechanism of Lu et al. We show that an optimized report-independent mixture is strategyproof and has a tight approximation ratio of $(74+4\sqrt{3})/23\approx 3.519$ on the line, improving upon the previous factor of $4$.

</details>


### [117] [Temporal Fair Division of Indivisible Mixed Manna: Tractable Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20033)
*Kui-Wang Choi,Minming Li,Nicholas Teh*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了不可分割混合甘露（兼有商品、苦差事和中立物品）的时间性公平分配问题。物品随时间到达且必须不可撤销地分配，要求每轮后的累积分配满足"最多嫉妒一个物品"（TEF1）。尽管即使只有商品，判断TEF1分配是否存在也是NP难的，但作者识别了若干可处理的情形：最多k种物品类型时在线循环规则保证EF⌈k/2⌉（因此至多两类时存在在线TEF1）；在特定缩放因子已知情况下可同时保证EF1和帕累托最优；两段到达序列且有共同排序时有TEF1规则；固定代理人数且值为有界整数时给出精确伪多项式算法。此外证明每个TEF1分配给予每个代理至少1/n的maximin份额（下界紧），且判断精确时间性maximin份额分配的存在性对商品和苦差事都是NP难的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的公平分配研究假设所有物品一次性同时分配，但现实中的许多场景（如云计算资源、在线广告位、众包任务等）物品是随时间动态到达的，需要在线实时决策。同时，物品对不同代理可能是商品、苦差事或中立物品（混合甘露），这比仅考虑商品更接近现实。作者希望探索在动态到达和不可撤销分配约束下，什么样的公平分配（TEF1）是可行的，并刻画其计算复杂度边界。

Method: 作者采用多种技术路线：（1）基于循环轮转的在线分配规则，针对物品类型数量k设计保证EF⌈k/2⌉的确定性在线算法；（2）在代理间按比例缩放达成一致的前提下，设计同时满足EF1和帕累托最优的在线规则；（3）针对两段到达序列与共同排序，设计特殊的分配策略；（4）对于固定代理数和有界整数估值，设计精确伪多项式算法；（5）通过构造反例证明maximin份额下界的紧性，并通过约简证明精确时间性maximin份额问题的NP难性。

Result: 主要结果包括：至多两类物品类型时存在在线TEF1分配；固定代理数和类型数时可在多项式时间内判断TEF1存在性；在缩放一致条件下可同时实现EF1和帕累托最优；两段到达且共同排序时可实现EF1；固定代理数且有界整数估值时存在精确伪多项式算法。同时证明了TEF1分配保证每个代理获得至少1/n的maximin份额且该界紧，以及精确时间性maximin份额分配的存在性判断对商品和苦差事均为NP难。

Conclusion: 本文系统地刻画了时间性公平分配（TEF1）的可处理性边界：虽然一般情形下NP难，但通过物品类型数限制、代理间偏好一致性假设、到达序列结构限制或估值范围限制可以获得可处理的算法。TEF1与maximin份额之间建立了紧密联系（1/n下界），同时揭示了时间性maximin份额问题的困难性。这些结果在在线公平分配领域开辟了新的研究方向。

Abstract: We study temporal fair division of indivisible mixed manna. Items arrive over time and must be allocated irrevocably; an item may be a good for some agents, a chore for others, and neutral for the rest. We require the cumulative allocation after every round to be envy-free up to one item (TEF1). Although deciding whether a TEF1 allocation exists is NP-hard even for goods, we identify several tractable settings. First, with at most $k$ item types, an online cyclic rule guarantees EF$\lceil k/2\rceil$ after every item arrival. Thus, every instance with at most two types admits an online TEF1 allocation; moreover, when the numbers of agents and types are fixed, TEF1 existence can be decided in polynomial time. Second, under agreement after agent-specific scaling, provided that the scaling factors are known before arrivals begin, an online rule produces an allocation that is EF1 and Pareto optimal after every item arrival. Third, for a two-part arrival sequence with common rankings, we give a rule that is EF1 after every item arrival. Fourth, when the number of agents is fixed and values are bounded integers, we give an exact pseudo-polynomial algorithm for deciding TEF1 existence. Finally, for goods, every TEF1 allocation gives each agent at least $1/n$ of her maximin share after every round; this factor is tight even for identical valuations and two rounds. Deciding whether an exact temporal maximin-share allocation exists is NP-hard for both goods and chores, even with identical valuations, two agents, and two rounds.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [GRACE: Grounded Reasoning via Adapter Composition and Evidence-Aware Calibration for Educational Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19355)
*Xinjin Li,Yudi Xia,Xi Zhao,Yiliu Xu,Yining Liu,Cheng Lu,Yujian Long,Yu Ma,Jinghan Cao,Liang Fan,Yeyun Xu*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出GRACE框架，利用教学状态信息（学科、技能、年级、视觉上下文等）为冻结的多模态大语言模型定制轻量级语言和视觉适配器，并通过证据感知的选项校准来提升教育场景下的视觉问答性能。在ScienceQA上，GRACE将总体准确率从90.5%提升至93.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教育视觉问答与常规开放式VQA不同，其题目包含结构化评估元数据、图形上下文和语义接近的选项，容易导致模型利用问题-选项间的捷径而忽视真正的视觉与语言推理。现有方法未充分利用这些丰富的教学状态信息。

Method: GRACE包含三个核心组件：(1) 因子特定提示（factor-specific prompts），利用教学状态中的学科、技能、年级、视觉上下文、问题意图和选项结构等信息生成定制化语言提示；(2) 轻量级视觉适配器，用于参数高效的视觉特征适配；(3) 证据感知的选项校准，在共享多模态上下文下对所有候选选项进行统一评分。

Result: 在ScienceQA上，GRACE相比共享适配器基线将总体准确率从90.5%提升至93.1%，图像上下文问题从88.7%提升至91.2%。消融实验显示移除教学状态组合、选项校准或视觉适配器分别导致总体准确率下降1.4、1.0和1.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 结构化教育状态是参数高效多模态适配的有效路由信号。通过教学状态驱动的适配器组合与证据感知校准，可以显著提升教育VQA性能，且消融实验验证了各组件均有实质贡献。

Abstract: Educational visual question answering, or VQA, requires models to solve curriculum-oriented multiple-choice questions using both language and visual evidence. Compared with conventional open-ended VQA, educational examples often include structured assessment metadata, diagrams or image contexts, and semantically close answer options, creating strong opportunities for question-option shortcuts. We develop and evaluate a parameter-efficient adaptation framework for a frozen multimodal large language model in this setting. We introduce GRACE, Grounded Reasoning via Adapter Composition and Evidence-Aware Calibration, a framework that uses the pedagogical state of each question to specialize lightweight language and vision adaptation. The state combines inference-visible subject, grouped skill, grade, visual-context, question-intent, and option-structure cues. GRACE uses factor-specific prompts and lightweight visual adapters, then applies evidence-aware option calibration to score all candidates under a shared multimodal context. On ScienceQA, GRACE improves a shared-adapter baseline from 90.5 percent to 93.1 percent overall accuracy and from 88.7 percent to 91.2 percent on image-context questions. Removing pedagogical composition, option calibration, or the visual adapter reduces overall accuracy by 1.4, 1.0, and 1.5 points, respectively. These controlled results show that structured educational state is an effective routing signal for parameter-efficient multimodal adaptation.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [CrossQ: Task-Aligned Cross-Token Conditional Quantization for Late Interaction Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19204)
*Rohit Kumar Salla,Manoj Saravanan,Ramya Manasa Amancherla*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CrossQ是一种针对晚期交互检索器（如ColBERT）的新型压缩方法，通过在索引时利用轻量级文档上下文来调节token编码，并采用与排名对齐的训练目标，在极低比特率（2-8 B/token）下大幅压缩多向量索引，同时保持甚至提升检索质量，实现了最高约64倍的存储压缩比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ColBERT等晚期交互检索器虽然检索质量高，但多向量索引体积庞大。现有标准的量化压缩方法以最小化token重建误差为目标，然而排名质量实际上取决于少数"胜出token"（winner tokens）的分数保留，二者目标不一致，导致压缩后检索质量下降明显。

Method: CrossQ在索引时利用轻量级文档上下文对token编码进行条件化调节（上下文字段不存储），以自适应的方式提升文档内有效token的保真度。训练采用与排名对齐的目标函数，同时保留候选分数分布并保护难负样本的边际。

Result: 在2 B/token下，CrossQ的MRR@10比最强纯足迹匹配量化基线高+0.010，比最强候选匹配系统基线高+0.012；在九个数据集的BEIR子集上，4 B/token时平均nDCG@10比最强候选匹配基线高+0.009。4 B/token下实现64倍原始token存储压缩，含元数据约61倍，保守对齐核算下约58倍。8 B/token下经轻量微调可保留全精度ColBERT MRR@10约98%。

Conclusion: CrossQ在晚期交互检索的存储压缩与检索质量之间取得了更优的权衡，通过排名感知的条件编码与对齐训练目标，在极低比特率下显著改善足迹-质量平衡，为内存受限的晚期交互检索场景提供了高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Late-interaction retrievers like ColBERT achieve high quality but suffer from large multi-vector indices. Standard compression minimizes token reconstruction error, while ranking depends critically on preserving scores of sparse "winner" tokens. We introduce CrossQ, which adaptively improves effective token fidelity within documents by conditioning token codes on lightweight document context computed at indexing time (but not stored). CrossQ is trained with ranking-aligned objectives that preserve candidate score distributions and protect hard-negative margins. At 2 B/token, CrossQ improves MRR@10 by +0.010 over the strongest strictly footprint-matched quantization baseline and by +0.012 over the strongest candidate-matched system reference. On a nine-dataset BEIR subset, CrossQ improves average nDCG@10 by +0.009 at 4 B/token over the strongest candidate-matched system reference. At 4 B/token, CrossQ achieves 64x raw token-storage reduction, approximately 61x including metadata and approximately 58x under conservative padding/alignment accounting. At 8 B/token, CrossQ with light fine-tuning retains approximately 98% of full-precision ColBERT MRR@10, improving the footprint-quality tradeoff for memory-constrained late-interaction retrieval.

</details>


### [120] [Quantization Beyond Uniform Bit Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19388)
*K. S. Sreeramji,Sabyasachi Basu,Ravishankar Krishnaswamy,Kirankumar Shiragur,Yujia Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可变比特分配框架，利用现代嵌入（特别是具有Matryoshka属性的嵌入）的几何结构，将嵌入划分为连续桶并采用非均匀存储分配，在相同内存预算下显著提升量化质量，PQ和SQ的召回率分别最高提升8%和18%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量化方案大多是嵌入无关的，在所有维度上均匀分配比特位，忽略了现代模型生成嵌入所具有的显著几何结构（如Matryoshka性质），导致固定内存预算下的量化效率低下。

Method: 提出一个简单的可变比特分配框架：将嵌入划分为连续桶，采用贪心分配策略在桶间非均匀分配存储资源。该框架分别实例化到产品量化（PQ）和标量量化（SQ）中。

Result: 在已知具有Matryoshka属性（MRL）的嵌入上进行实验，非均匀分配在相同存储预算下始终优于均匀基线。在相同压缩率下，可变分配使PQ召回率最高提升8%，SQ最高提升18%。最大的改进出现在低比特率区域。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，利用嵌入几何结构的可变比特分配为大规模检索系统的结构感知压缩与索引技术开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Quantization is a fundamental technique to handle the growing sizes of embeddings generated by modern models. Existing quantization schemes are largely embedding agnostic and allocate bits uniformly across dimensions. However, recent models produce embeddings with significant geometric structure. In this work, we investigate whether a variable bit allocation scheme can improve quantization quality under a fixed memory budget. We propose a simple variable bit allocation framework that partitions an embedding into contiguous buckets and allocates storage non-uniformly across them. Using a greedy allocation strategy, we instantiate this framework for both Product Quantization (PQ) and Scalar Quantization (SQ). We perform a series of experiments on embeddings known to have the Matryoshka property (MRL), and consistently observe that non-uniform allocations outperform uniform baselines at identical storage budgets. The largest improvements occur in the low-bit regime, where uniform allocation is particularly inefficient for MRL embeddings. At the same compression rates, variable allocation improves recall by up to 8\% for PQ and up to 18\% for SQ. Our results suggest a new direction for structure-aware compression and indexing techniques for large-scale retrieval systems.

</details>


### [121] [HARP: Hierarchical Adaptive Ranking with Preference-Adaptive Fusion for Query-Based CVE Prioritization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19430)
*Haochen Liu,Zhengzhang Chen,Haoyu Wang,Yanchi Liu,Jundong Li,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出HARP框架，用于在偏好场景隐含的情况下进行基于查询的CVE优先级排序。HARP利用漏洞知识图谱和带标签的历史示例作为支撑库，通过策略条件下的全局、企业和用户三个视图对候选CVE进行评分，并从支撑样本中学习视图融合权重，无需显式文本描述当前偏好场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 漏洞优先级排序本质上是偏好依赖的，同一CVE在不同操作偏好场景下应有不同的修复优先级。然而现有评分系统假设固定标准，组织的偏好往往是隐含的、难以用提示词显式表达，而历史已验证的排期案例更容易获得。因此需要一种无需显式偏好描述、仅依靠历史标注案例来引导优先级排序的方法。

Method: HARP是一个图基多视图框架：(1) 从漏洞知识图谱中检索证据；(2) 通过策略条件下的全局、企业和用户三个视图对候选进行评分；(3) 从支撑样本中拟合视图融合权重，以实现对当前偏好场景的隐式建模。

Result: 在三个偏好场景和多个骨干大语言模型上的实验表明，HARP超越了多个基线方法，验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: HARP通过利用历史标注案例作为隐式偏好信号的来源，结合知识图谱多视图评分和自适应权重融合，有效解决了偏好场景难以显式表达时的CVE优先级排序问题。

Abstract: Vulnerability prioritization is inherently preference dependent, since the same CVE can receive different remediation priority under different operational preference scenarios. Existing scoring systems and ranking methods typically assume a fixed criterion. In practice, organizations already operate under a preference scenario, but this preference is often implicit and difficult to express as a written prompt instruction, while triage queries usually do not encode it. Past validated triage cases under the current scenario are more readily available. We study query-based CVE prioritization in this setting and propose HARP, a graph-grounded multi-view framework that ranks candidates from a natural-language query together with a support bank of historical labeled examples from the current preference scenario, without requiring an explicit textual summary of that scenario. HARP retrieves evidence from a vulnerability knowledge graph, scores candidates with policy-conditioned global, enterprise, and user views, and fits view-fusion weights from sampled supports. Experiments across three preference scenarios and multiple backbone LLMs show that HARP outperforms multiple baselines, expressing our method's effectiveness.

</details>


### [122] [SSR-GRPO: Integrating Supervision and Semantic IDs into Reinforcement Learning for Dense Retrieval in E-commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19595)
*Guangxin Song,Xing Fang,Mingmin Jin,Jing Wang,Bokang Wang,Zhentao Song,Junjie Bai,Jianbo Zhu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出SSR-GRPO方法，针对电子商务搜索中嵌入检索（EBR）在处理复杂语义方面的不足，通过语义标识符（SIDs）进行双重视角相关性评估、利用SIDs层次关系挖掘难负样本、并结合掩蔽机制的R-GRPO和Retrieval-DPO任务，提升检索性能，已在大型电商平台部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EBR方法在处理复杂和隐式语义时能力不足；R-GRPO虽引入强化学习到密集检索，但受限于批量采样导致top-K候选噪声大，且使用相似训练的LLM作为奖励模型会造成相关性评估偏差。

Method: 提出SSR-GRPO，包含三个核心部分：(1) 双重视角框架，结合量化学习产生的语义标识符（SIDs）和密集表示向量来生成更无偏的相关性评分；(2) 利用SIDs的层次相似关系挖掘难负样本，并设计掩蔽函数集成到R-GRPO中过滤组内噪声样本；(3) 构建Retrieval-DPO任务，通过正负样本对让模型从成对角度捕捉细粒度语义差异。

Result: 大量离线与在线实验验证了SSR-GRPO的有效性，并已成功部署在大型电商平台上。

Conclusion: SSR-GRPO通过语义标识符增强相关性评估、难负样本挖掘和成对优化策略，有效解决了R-GRPO的噪声和偏差问题，提升了电商搜索中复杂语义的检索效果，且具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: Embedding-based retrieval (EBR) is pivotal in e-commerce search but often struggles with complex semantics. While recent methods often fine-tune large language models (LLMs) for representation learning, they typically lack robust mechanisms for handling complex and implicit semantics. While Retrieval-GRPO (R-GRPO) recently introduced reinforcement learning to dense retrieval, it suffers from noisy top-K candidates due to limited batch sampling and biased relevance assessments caused by using similarly trained LLMs as reward models. To tackle these issues, we propose Supervised Retrieval-GRPO with Semantic Identifiers (SSR-GRPO). Specifically, our method first proposes a dual-perspective framework for relevance assessment. It leverages both Semantic Identifiers (SIDs) produced by quantization learning and dense representation vectors to generate more unbiased relevance scores. Furthermore, leveraging the hierarchical similarity relationships of the generated SIDs, we mine a set of hard negative samples that serve two purposes: (1) to design a masking function integrated into R-GRPO, effectively filtering intra-group noisy samples; and (2) to construct a Retrieval-DPO task composed of positive and negative sample pairs, enabling the model to capture fine-grained semantic distinctions from a pair-wise perspective. By integrating these optimization strategies, we propose SSR-GRPO. Extensive offline and online experiments validate SSR-GRPO's effectiveness, and it has been deployed on a large-scale e-commerce platform.

</details>


### [123] [Training-Free LLM-Based Recommendation with Post-LLM Item Refinement Using Collaborative Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19665)
*Kyungho Kim,Sunwoo Kim,Geon Lee,Shinhwan Kang,Sojeong Kim,Liam Collins,Bhuvesh Kumar,Donald Loveland,Kijung Shin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CoRRe是一个无需训练的推荐框架，采用"后LLM"范式，将协同过滤信号注入LLM生成的物品表示中，再与LLM生成的用户兴趣进行匹配排序，在真实数据集上超越现有无需训练的方法，并接近或超越基于训练的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的用户兴趣往往过于宽泛，难以进行细粒度物品检索；现有方法在LLM之前（pre-LLM）融合协同过滤信号，通过候选重排序或提示增强等方式，收益有限。

Method: 提出CoRRe框架，采用后LLM（post-LLM）范式：利用物品-物品共同购买图来精炼物品嵌入的方向，利用物品流行度来精炼嵌入的幅度，然后将精炼后的物品表示与LLM生成的用户兴趣进行匹配排序。

Result: 在真实数据集上的实验表明，CoRRe一致地优于现有的无需训练方法，并且与基于训练的方法相比达到竞争性或更优的性能，且无需任何模型训练或任务特定微调。

Conclusion: CoRRe证明了在后LLM阶段注入协同过滤信号是有效的，能够在不进行训练的情况下显著提升基于LLM的推荐的细粒度检索性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for training-free recommendation, but LLM-generated user interests are often too broad for fine-grained item retrieval. Existing methods incorporate collaborative filtering (CF) signals in a pre-LLM manner through candidate reranking or prompt augmentation, yielding limited gains. We propose CoRRe, a training-free recommendation framework with a post-LLM paradigm that injects CF signals into LLM-generated item representations, which are later matched with LLM-generated user interests for ranking. Specifically, CoRRe refines the directions of item embeddings using an item-item co-purchase graph and their magnitudes using item popularity. Experiments on real-world datasets show that CoRRe consistently outperforms existing training-free methods and achieves competitive or superior performance compared with training-based methods, without requiring any model training or task-specific fine-tuning.

</details>


### [124] [Do Sequential Recommendation Benchmarks Really Require Higher-Order Sequence Modelling?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19833)
*Aleksandr V. Petrov,Praveen Chandar,Paul N. Bennett,Hugues Bouchard,Mounia Lalmas*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文通过两个简单的近因加权成对探针（SeqRules和PCTM），发现广泛使用的序列推荐基准数据集（如Amazon数据集和MovieLens-1M）无法有效衡量高阶序列建模能力——这些简单探针在多个数据集上甚至超越了基于Transformer的SASRec模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列推荐系统越来越多地采用语言模型架构来捕捉复杂的上下文相关交互，但现有的广泛使用的基准测试是否真的需要这种建模能力仍不明确。作者希望检验这些基准能否真正区分高阶序列建模模型与简单方法。

Method: 提出两个简单的近因加权成对探针：Sequential Rules (SeqRules)和Probabilistic Collaborative Transition Model (PCTM)。这两个探针不学习高阶序列表示，仅基于近因加权成对交互进行预测。在eSASRec和SASRec的评估协议下，与Transformer-based模型进行对比实验。

Result: 在三个Amazon数据集上，至少一个探针超过eSASRec复现结果15-38%；在MovieLens-1M上超过4.4%，但在MovieLens-20M上落后27.3%。在其余四个数据集中，至少一个探针也超过采样softmax SASRec复现结果9-28%。

Conclusion: 广泛使用的序列推荐基准数据集不适合衡量高阶序列建模所带来的增益。将Transformer模型与强近因加权成对探针进行比较，可以作为检验基准是否能有效衡量高阶序列建模增益的具体测试方法。

Abstract: Sequential recommenders increasingly use language-model architectures designed to capture complex, context-dependent interactions. Yet it remains unclear whether widely used benchmarks actually require this modelling capacity. We investigate this question using two simple, recency-weighted pairwise probes that do not learn higher-order sequence representations: Sequential Rules (SeqRules) and our Probabilistic Collaborative Transition Model (PCTM). Using the evaluation protocol of eSASRec, at least one probe exceeds our eSASRec reproduction by 15-38% on three Amazon datasets and by 4.4% on MovieLens-1M, but trails it by 27.3% on MovieLens-20M. On the four remaining datasets, at least one probe also outperforms our sampled-softmax SASRec reproduction by 9-28%, suggesting that these widely used benchmarks are poorly suited to measuring gains from higher-order sequence modelling. More broadly, comparing Transformer-based models against strong recency-weighted pairwise probes provides a concrete test of whether a benchmark can meaningfully measure gains from higher-order sequence modelling.

</details>


### [125] [SCoRD: Semantic-Assisted Continual Retriever-Reranker Distillation for LLM-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19998)
*Seunghyun Baek,Gyuseok Lee,Seunghan Lee,Wonbin Kweon,Dong Wang,SeongKu Kang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SCoRD是一个用于LLM重排序推荐管道的持续知识蒸馏框架，通过语义推理助手将LLM的用户意图推断能力蒸馏成可复用的意图级指导，在非平稳数据流下实现检索器与重排序器的高效协同适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统采用两阶段流程（ID检索器+LLM重排序器），但实际部署中需持续适应不断变化的用户兴趣和新交互。反复更新LLM重排序器并蒸馏其知识代价过高，而仅更新检索器又因容量有限难以从稀疏数据中学习。需要一个既能高效协同适应两端、又避免昂贵重复推理的解决方案。

Method: SCoRD框架包含三个核心机制：(1) 语义推理助手——将LLM推断用户潜在意图的能力蒸馏为可复用的意图级指导；(2) 选择性蒸馏——仅在低置信度序列上将重排序器知识蒸馏给检索器，避免盲目蒸馏；(3) 双向反馈——在无重复LLM推理的情况下引导检索器更新，同时将检索器表示和意图漂移信号反馈给重排序器。

Result: 在真实数据集上的实验表明，SCoRD能够在非平稳数据流下实现有效且高效的检索器-重排序器协同适应，平衡了适应质量与计算成本。

Conclusion: SCoRD验证了通过意图级知识蒸馏和选择性更新策略，可以在避免重复LLM推理代价的同时，实现检索器与重排序器在持续数据流下的高效协同进化，为实际部署中的LLM推荐管道提供了可行的持续适应方案。

Abstract: Recommendation systems increasingly adopt a two-stage pipeline, where an ID-based retriever retrieves candidates and an LLM-based reranker refines their rankings. To improve retrieval quality, reranker-to-retriever distillation is commonly used to transfer the reranker's knowledge to the retriever. For practical deployment, however, this pipeline must continually adapt to evolving interests and incoming interactions. A naive solution is to repeatedly update the LLM reranker and distill its latest knowledge, but this incurs prohibitive costs. Updating the retriever alone is cheaper, but its limited capacity makes adaptation from sparse data difficult. We propose SCoRD, a continual knowledge distillation framework for LLM-based reranking pipelines under a non-stationary data stream. SCoRD introduces a semantic reasoning assistant that distills the LLM's ability to infer underlying user intents into reusable intent-level guidance. It selectively distills reranker knowledge to the retriever on low-confidence sequences, guides retriever-only updates without repeated LLM inference, and feeds retriever-derived representations and intent-drift signals back to the reranker. Experiments on real-world datasets show that SCoRD enables effective and efficient retriever-reranker co-adaptation.

</details>


### [126] [Daedalus-150M: A Convolution-Attention Hybrid Designed for CPU Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20210)
*Christos Koutsiaris*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种反向设计的小语言模型：先固定目标（单用户、单token、4-bit权重、普通CPU），再选择适配的架构。模型18个块中仅6个使用全注意力，其余12个使用短卷积（内存宽度仅两个时间步，不随对话长度增长）。在59.9B tokens上从头训练，在五项基准上得47.31分（预设定门槛42.20），超越GPT-2 124M等更大数据训练的模型，并超过MobileLLM-125M。相比同规模全注意力模型，混合架构在质量上略优、4-bit文件小6.3%、解码速度快1.76-2.08倍，且速度优势随上下文长度增长。作者还报告了失败经验：未缓解的4-bit质量损失、约半数卷积通道失效且无法移除、词汇表过大等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统小语言模型通常先按大模型架构设计，再压缩到CPU上运行。作者采取相反思路：先锁定CPU部署的硬约束（4-bit权重、单用户单token推理），再据此选择架构，旨在解决小模型在长上下文场景下缓存重新读取导致的效率瓶颈。

Method: 采用混合架构：18个块中6个保留全注意力，12个使用短卷积（内存仅两个时间步宽，不随对话增长而扩大），使三分之二的网络无需重新读取增长的缓存。模型从头训练于59.9B tokens，并训练了同规模全注意力模型作为对照，预先设定获胜条件以避免选择偏差。

Result: 五任务基准得分47.31，超过预先设定的42.20门槛；超越GPT-2 124M、Pythia-160M、OPT-125M、GPT-neo-125M及MobileLLM-125M。验证集bits-per-byte为0.8685。混合模型在质量上优于对照0.81%，4-bit文件小6.3%，解码速度在2048 tokens上下文下快1.76倍（外部对比2.08倍），速度优势随上下文长度增长。带宽计算仅预测1.17x，说明内存体积不是全部原因。

Conclusion: 针对目标硬件约束反向设计架构是有效的：混合注意力-卷积架构能在CPU上实现与更大模型相当的质量和更优的速度。但研究也揭示了未解决的问题：4-bit量化有无法忽略的质量代价、部分卷积通道无效且难以剪除、词汇表大小超出模型规模所需。

Abstract: Small language models are usually built like large ones and then squeezed onto a CPU afterwards. We did the opposite: we fixed the target first, one user, one token at a time, 4-bit weights, ordinary CPU, and chose the architecture to suit it. The result keeps full attention in only 6 of its 18 blocks. The other 12 use short convolutions whose memory is two timesteps wide no matter how long the conversation gets, so two thirds of the network never re-reads a growing cache.
  Trained from scratch on 59.9B tokens, the model scores 47.31 on a five-task benchmark against a bar of 42.20 that was fixed before training began. It beats GPT-2 124M, Pythia-160M, OPT-125M and GPT-neo-125M, all trained on three to six times more data, and exceeds MobileLLM-125M's published score despite that model seeing a trillion tokens. Validation bits-per-byte is 0.8685.
  To check the architecture rather than the training recipe, we trained a conventional all-attention model of the same size on the same data, and wrote down the winning condition before scoring either. The hybrid won the chosen quality metric by 0.81%, matched it on downstream tasks, produced a 6.3% smaller 4-bit file, and decoded 1.76x faster at 2048 tokens of context, 2.08x against an external model of similar size. In every measurement the speed advantage is near zero at an empty context and grows with length, which is what the mechanism predicts and what a merely leaner model would not show. A simple bandwidth calculation predicts only 1.17x, so memory volume alone does not explain the gap.
  We also report what did not work: an unmitigated 4-bit quality cost, roughly half the convolution channels ending up inert and impossible to remove, and a vocabulary larger than this model size warrants.

</details>


### [127] [What Makes a Good Fiqh Retriever? Answer Retrieval for Arabic Islamic Jurisprudence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20246)
*Somaya Eltanbouly,Heba Sbahi,Samer Rashwani,Abdessalam Bouchekif,Mutaz al-Khatib,Shahd Gaben,Mohammed Ghaly*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究了检索增强生成（RAG）在阿拉伯伊斯兰教法（fiqh）问答中的应用，聚焦于"携带答案的检索"问题。作者构建了一个阿拉伯语fiqh检索测试集，评估了密集、词法、混合、微调和教法学派感知等多种检索策略。最佳检索器达到0.524 MRR@5，微调后提升至0.553，教法学派感知过滤在学派特定问题上将MRR@5提高了一倍以上。错误分析表明主要挑战在于区分携带答案的段落与主题相似但不含所需裁决的段落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有伊斯兰教法问答系统大多进行端到端评估，无法将检索失败与生成失败分离，导致检索环节的问题难以定位和优化。

Method: 构建阿拉伯语fiqh检索测试集，系统比较密集、词法、混合、微调及教法学派感知等多种检索策略，并进行错误分析。

Result: 最佳检索器MRR@5为0.524，微调提升至0.553；混合检索对强模型增益有限；教法学派感知过滤在学派相关问题上将MRR@5提高超过两倍。

Conclusion: fiqh检索的主要挑战是区分携带答案的段落与主题相似但不含所需裁决的段落，教法学派感知方法能显著提升特定问题的检索效果。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation is used for Islamic question answering, but most systems are evaluated end-to-end, making retrieval failures difficult to isolate from generation failures. We study answer-bearing retrieval for Arabic fiqh, where a passage is relevant only if it states the ruling required by the question. We build a retrieval test collection for Arabic fiqh and use it to evaluate dense, lexical, hybrid, fine-tuned, and madhhab-aware retrieval strategies. The best retriever achieves 0.524 MRR@5, while fine-tuning improves performance to 0.553. Hybrid retrieval provides limited gains for strong models, whereas madhhab-aware filtering more than doubles MRR@5 on school-specific questions. We further present an error analysis showing that the main challenge is distinguishing answer-bearing passages from topically similar passages that do not contain the requested ruling.

</details>


### [128] [Projecting BrowseComp-Plus onto ClimbMix: Toward More Realistic Corpora for Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20317)
*Sahel Sharifymoghaddam,Lingwei Gu,Yijun Ge,Jimmy Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍 BrowseComp-Plus_CM 基准，通过投影管道将原有基准问题重新定位到 NVIDIA 发布的 400B token 的 ClimbMix 语料库上，将智能体搜索评估与检索器解耦，最终从 830 个问题中筛选出 57 个完全有据可依的问题，暴露出检索已成为主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 BrowseComp-Plus 基准虽将智能体搜索与检索器解耦，但其语料库仅约 10 万篇文档，且证据与干扰项均针对查询预先选取，评估存在偏差。需要一个与基准无关的、大规模的真实语料库来重新定位问题证据，以更公平地分离智能体能力与检索器能力。

Method: 提出投影管道：1) 将每个问题分解为原子推理跳数；2) 在 ClimbMix（400B token、553M 文档的预训练语料）中为每跳寻找证据；3) 通过自动验证、独立智能体和人工评审三层确认每跳均有支撑；4) 只保留全部通过的问题。该管道与数据集无关，适用于任何可分解为可验证事实的基准问题。

Result: 从 830 个 BrowseComp-Plus 测试问题中筛选出 57 个完全有据可依的问题。最强智能体的答案准确率下降 5 个百分点，证据召回率从 84.3% 骤降至 21.4%，同时搜索调用次数增加 63%，表明投影将难度转移到了检索环节。

Conclusion: 投影后的基准将挑战重心转移到检索上，证实了大规模真实语料中的证据定位是当前智能体搜索的主要瓶颈。作为一系列投影工作的首个成果，作者开源了管道、基准和分析结果。

Abstract: The BrowseComp-Plus benchmark disentangled the evaluation of agentic search by replacing opaque web search with a fixed corpus, so that an agent's role can be separated from the retriever's. That corpus, however, holds only about 100K documents and was assembled from the supporting documents of the benchmark's own queries plus mined hard negatives, so the evidence and the distractors were both selected per query. We introduce $\text{BrowseComp-Plus}_{\text{CM}}$, which keeps the BrowseComp-Plus questions but relocates their evidence to ClimbMix, a 400B-token, 553M-document mixture of web text released by NVIDIA for pre-training language models and built without reference to any benchmark. Our main contribution is the projection pipeline that makes this possible: it decomposes each question into atomic reasoning hops and grounds every hop in the new corpus, retaining a question only when automatic verification, an independent agent, and human review all confirm that every hop is supported. The pipeline is dataset-agnostic and applies to any benchmark whose questions decompose into verifiable facts. Applied to the 830 BrowseComp-Plus test questions, our pipeline yields 57 fully grounded questions with question-level relevance judgments. Projection shifts the difficulty onto retrieval, as the strongest agent we evaluate loses five points of answer accuracy but sees its evidence recall fall from 84.3% to 21.4% while issuing 63% more search calls. As the first of a series of projections, we release the pipeline, the benchmark, and our analyses at https://github.com/castorini/cmass.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [Towards On-Board Implementation of ML-Based Helicopter Weight Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19210)
*Nicolas Valot,Ammar Mechouche,Benjamin Lesage,Claire Pagetti,Louis Fabre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于监督式机器学习模型的直升机起飞重量估算方法，利用空客全球在役机队的大规模数据集。研究遵循EASA机器学习应用概念文件和EUROCAE ED-324标准的适航学习保证流程，提出了机器学习需求、模型描述及其实现，并在传统航电计算机上验证了LSTM循环神经网络的适用性，证明可用于机载关键功能（如机载告警）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直升机起飞重量是影响飞行安全的关键参数，涉及告警等关键功能。传统的重量估算方法可能不够精确或无法适应飞行数据的复杂性，因此需要利用机器学习方法结合大规模真实在役数据来提升估算精度，并确保该方法符合适航认证要求。

Method: 本文采用监督式机器学习方法，具体使用长短期记忆（LSTM）循环神经网络来估算直升机起飞重量。研究提出了完整的学习保证流程，包括：（1）定义符合EASA概念文件和EUROCAE ED-324标准的机器学习需求；（2）编写机器学习模型描述；（3）在传统航电计算机上实现LSTM模型；（4）对实现进行需求验证。

Result: 研究成功在传统航线计算机上实现了LSTM重量估算器，验证结果表明该实现满足所提出的机器学习需求，适用于在机载目标上部署，可用于直升机起飞重量估算及关键告警功能。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了机器学习模型在航空适航认证框架下的可行性与实用性，为未来在关键机载功能中安全部署深度学习模型提供了方法论参考和实践验证。

Abstract: This paper focuses on the implementation of a novel supervised Machine Learning model for estimating helicopter weight during takeoff, utilizing extensive datasets from Airbus's global in-service fleet. The study details a learning assurance process aligned with the EASA concept paper for machine learning application, and with the on-going Eurocae ED-324. We propose a set of Machine Learning Requirements, a Machine Learning Model Description, and its implementation for a long short-term memory recurrent neural network. Finally, we verify the requirements on the implementation. Demonstrated on legacy avionics computers, the implementation is suitable for the deployment of the developed Machine Learning Model weight estimator on airborne targets for critical functions such as on-board alerting.

</details>


### [130] [Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19234)
*Eddy Soria,Aida Valls,Ana Beatriz Hernández-Lara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的三角模糊数距离度量（d_{TR}），该度量将线性缩放直接集成到距离计算中，无需前期归一化步骤即可处理异构属性的不同尺度和单位，并通过数学证明其满足度量性质（非负性、同一性、对称性、三角不等式）以及有界性、尺度不变性和原点不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模糊距离度量假设所有值处于同一尺度，当应用于具有不同尺度或单位的异构属性时，需要额外的预归一化阶段。这增加了计算复杂性，也可能引入误差。因此需要一种能直接在距离计算中处理归一化问题的度量方法。

Method: 提出d_{TR}距离度量，将线性重缩放（LRE）直接集成到模糊数比较的距离计算过程中，使归一化在比较时自动完成。论文对d_{TR}的度量性质（非负性、同一性、对称性、三角不等式）进行了形式化证明，并验证其有界性、尺度不变性和原点不变性。

Result: d_{TR}被证明是满足完整度量公理的度量函数，同时具有有界性、尺度不变性和原点不变性；通过引入维度权重向量，可优先处理不同维度的重要性，适用于异构模糊数据场景。

Conclusion: d_{TR}为异构模糊数据的距离度量提供了无需预归一化的解决方案，适用于合成指标构建、基于距离的机器学习算法和多准则决策辅助等领域。

Abstract: Decision-making in complex systems often involves dealing with imprecise or uncertain information, frequently represented using fuzzy sets, particularly Triangular Fuzzy Numbers (TFNs). A crucial aspect of many fuzzy methods is the quantification of distance between TFNs. Many distance measures assume that all values are in the same scale, requiring a preliminary normalization stage when applied to heterogeneous attributes with different scales or units. This paper proposes the Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance (d_{TR}), a metric designed to address this challenge. The d_{TR} uniquely integrates Linear Rescaling (LRE) directly into the distance calculation, ensuring normalization during the comparison of fuzzy numbers. We formally prove that d_{TR} satisfies the properties of a metric, including non-negativity, identity, symmetry, and the triangle inequality. Furthermore, we demonstrate that d_{TR} is bounded, scale-invariant, and origin-invariant. These properties, combined with a weighting vector for prioritizing dimensions, make d_{TR} suitable for applications involving heterogeneous fuzzy data, such as the construction of synthetic indicators, distance-based machine learning algorithms or multicriteria-decision aiding.

</details>


### [131] [Holtercare-Bench: A Multimodal Benchmark for Evaluating Long-Term Dynamic ECG Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19297)
*Yihan Xie,Hanwen Cui,Runze Ye,Juekai Lin,Haoyang Wang,Jinhao Mao,Bo Zhang,Wenqiao Zhang,Xiaogang Guo,Jun Xiao,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Holtercare-23K大型多模态动态心电图数据集（含22,980个QA对，源自788份临床Holter记录，具有信号-视频-文本三模态对齐）及Holtercare-Bench基准，用于评估MLLM在超长病理序列上的时间定位、临床诊断和全局总结能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在医学应用中主要偏向静态图像或短期信号，在处理动态心电图的复杂时间推理和诊断报告生成方面表现不佳，且缺乏高质量数据集和基准。作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构建Holtercare-23K数据集（信号-视频-文本三模态对齐），并基于此提出Holtercare-Bench基准，涵盖时间定位、临床诊断和全局总结三大评测任务；通过零样本评测与微调对比验证模型性能。

Result: 零样本评测表明领先多模态大模型在处理超长病理序列时存在显著性能差距；对代表性模型进行微调后取得大幅提升。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了当前MLLM在电生理学领域的局限性，并为长期医学MLLM提供了基础性基准，为推动动态心电信号时序理解与报告生成研究奠定基础。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) excel in medical applications, most of them favor static images or short-term signals. In the critical field of dynamic electrocardiograms (ECG), models struggle with complex temporal reasoning and diagnostic report generation due to a lack of high-quality datasets and benchmarks. To address this, we introduce (i) Holtercare-23K, a large-scale multimodal dynamic ECG dataset comprising 22,980 QA pairs derived from 788 clinical Holter records and featuring a novel signal-video-text tri-modal alignment. Based on this dataset, we present (ii) Holtercare-Bench, a multimodal benchmark that evaluates models on temporal localization, clinical diagnosis, and global summarization. Zero-shot evaluations of leading MLLMs reveal a significant performance gap in processing ultra-long pathological sequences. However, fine-tuning representative models yields substantial improvements. This work illuminates the limitations of current MLLMs in electrophysiology and provides a foundational benchmark for long-term medical MLLMs. Our project is available at https://github.com/ZJU4HealthCare/Holtercare-Bench.

</details>


### [132] [Quantum Kernel Estimation for the Discovery of Early Lung Cancer Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19304)
*Hamed Javidi,Alex Zajichek,Hakan Doga,Laxmi Parida,Filippo Utro,Peter J. Mazzone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了量子-经典混合机器学习用于基于cfDNA的肺癌检测，使用DNA片段组学和DNA甲基化数据，通过量子核方法与经典SVM对比，发现量子核模型在片段组学上表现有竞争力，在甲基化上经典SVM最优但量子模型仍具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺癌筛查虽然能降低死亡率，但受限于筛查参与率、依从性和管理挑战。基于cfDNA的血液生物标志物提供了补充途径，但由于肺癌异质性和高维非线性分子信号，早期检测仍然困难，需要探索更强大的机器学习方法。

Method: 使用DNA片段组学和DNA甲基化数据，经特征选择后采用20和40特征子集训练模型。将特征编码到量子希尔伯特空间，使用角度和密集角度特征映射及多种纠缠策略，通过精确态矢量模拟计算基于保真度的量子核，与预计算核SVM和核PCA逻辑回归集成，并与原始特征训练的SVM基线比较。

Result: 在重复的保留验证中，量子核模型在两组数据集上均取得有竞争力的性能。片段组学方面，多个20特征配置的AUC优于经典SVM基线；甲基化方面，经典SVM获得最高AUC，但部分量子模型仍具竞争力且在某些情况下提高特异性。特征从20增至40并未持续改善性能，反而增加了变异性。

Conclusion: 量子核方法是一种有前景的cfDNA肺癌检测方法，该框架系统评估了编码和纠缠设计对分类的影响，显示量子核模型能够有效捕捉cfDNA片段化的非线性结构。

Abstract: Lung cancer screening with low-dose chest computed tomography reduces mortality, but its impact is limited by uptake, adherence, and management challenges. Blood-based cell-free DNA (cfDNA) biomarkers offer a complementary approach, although early detection remains difficult because of lung cancer heterogeneity and high-dimensional, nonlinear molecular signals. We evaluated quantum-classical hybrid machine learning for lung cancer detection using DNA fragmentomics and DNA methylation. After feature selection, models were trained using 20- and 40-feature subsets. Features were encoded into quantum Hilbert space using angle and dense-angle feature maps with multiple entanglement strategies. Fidelity-based quantum kernels were computed with exact statevector simulation and integrated with precomputed-kernel SVM and kernel-PCA logistic regression and compared with an SVM model trained on the original features.
  This framework enabled systematic evaluation of how encoding and entanglement design affect classification. Across repeated held-out evaluations, quantum-kernel models achieved competitive performance on both datasets. For fragmentomics, several 20-feature configurations improved AUC relative to a classical SVM baseline, suggesting effective capture of nonlinear cfDNA fragmentation structure. For methylation, the classical SVM achieved the highest AUC, although selected quantum models remained competitive and improved specificity in some cases. Increasing features from 20 to 40 did not consistently improve performance and often increased variability. Overall, these results support quantum kernel methods as a promising approach for cfDNA-based lung cancer detection.

</details>


### [133] [Evidence Before Expansion: Reuse, Spawn, or Defer in Lifelong Expert Pools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19888)
*Kentaro Oda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个决策层，用于流式专家模型池系统，使"复用专家"、"创建新专家"和"延迟决策"三种结果都具有统计意义。通过配对赌e-process和重启式e-detector，实现了有限时间随时有效性的保证，在多个基准数据集上达到零误判效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式系统中维护专家模型池时，需要反复决定是否复用现有专家、创建新专家或延迟决策。现有方法缺乏统计意义上的严谨性，无法保证三种决策结果的合理性。

Method: 提出一个决策层：(1) 将复用和创建建模为单侧序贯假设检验，以条件机制级差异为目标，用不关心区域分离；(2) 延迟恰好是两种赌e-process都未积累足够证据的状态；(3) 使用重启式e-detector，在几何间隔的重启时间点维护无窗口赌超鞅组（O(log t)内存），误差预算在重启实例间分配；(4) 通过可预测判别器序列实现可观测替代差异的有限时间随时有效性证明。

Result: 在合成多概念流、Electricity、Covertype和INSECTS基准上，实例计入的重启式银行在切换后实现零虚假创建和零虚假复用，性能匹配或超越退休的窗口启发式方法（INSECTS-重现准确率0.675）。

Conclusion: 部署算法与有保证的算法实现统一，重启式e-detector在保持终生随时有效性的同时实现了近期性（recency），并通过在专家创建顺序上分配误差预算来控制无限多专家的多重性问题。

Abstract: Streaming systems that maintain a pool of expert models must repeatedly decide whether to reuse an existing expert for arriving data, spawn a new one, or defer. We present a decision layer that makes all three outcomes statistically meaningful. Reuse and spawn are posed as one-sided sequential hypotheses on a conditional (mechanism-level) discrepancy, separated by an indifference zone; defer is exactly the state in which neither betting e-process has accumulated sufficient evidence. We prove finite-time anytime validity for the observable surrogate discrepancy of a predictable discriminator sequence, and an unconditional one-sided transfer to the population quantity in which each side's slack is the excess risk of a single discriminator; an empirically observed downward-bias regularity makes the spawn side exactly conservative. Recency without sacrificing the guarantee is obtained by a restarted e-detector: a bank of unwindowed betting supermartingales at geometrically spaced restart times (O(log t) memory), with the error budget spent over restart instances, which preserves lifetime anytime validity; spending over expert-creation order likewise controls multiplicity for unboundedly many experts. On synthetic multi-concept streams, Electricity, Covertype, and the recurrence-heavy INSECTS benchmark, the instance-accounted restarted bank achieves zero false spawns and zero false reuses after switches and matches or exceeds the retired windowed heuristic (INSECTS-reoccurring accuracy 0.675), making the deployed algorithm and the guaranteed algorithm one and the same.

</details>


### [134] [Improved Confidence Estimates for Black-Box Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19323)
*Sokhna Diarra Mbacke,Mouloud Belbahri,Gabriel Loaiza-Ganem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用数据集来提升大语言模型（LLM）不确定性量化（UQ）性能的方法。该方法通过构建简单的分类器，将现有的UQ分数和相似查询的正确性作为特征来预测LLM回答的正确性，从而实现比现有零样本方法更优的性能，且计算开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM不确定性量化方法（如口头化置信度、多轮生成等）大多是零样本的，无需标注数据即可量化不确定性。然而在实际部署前，研究人员总会在目标数据集上评估这些方法的性能。作者认为，如果能够利用这一评估阶段的数据集信息，就能持续超越现有的零样本UQ方法，从而提升部署的安全性。

Method: 该方法的核心是构建简单的分类器来预测LLM回答的正确性。具体做法是：利用现有的UQ分数（如口头化置信度等）以及相似查询的正确性信息作为分类器的特征，然后训练分类器来预测LLM回答是否正确。该方法将UQ从零样本评分问题转化为一个监督学习问题，充分利用了部署前数据集评估阶段的信息。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在所有测试场景中一致地优于现有的UQ评分方法。该方法引入了极小的计算开销，使其成为实际应用中LLM不确定性量化的低成本、简便的增强方案。

Conclusion: 通过利用部署前数据集中的信息，将UQ转化为一个简单的监督分类任务，可以持续超越零样本UQ方法，同时保持极低的计算成本，为LLM在实际应用中的安全部署提供了一种实用的增强方案。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) is essential for the safe deployment of large language models (LLMs). Existing methods, from verbalized confidence to ones requiring multiple generations, are often zero-shot and produce scores quantifying uncertainty without the need for labelled data. Nonetheless, in practice one must always evaluate their performance on a dataset of interest before deployment. In this work we show that, by leveraging this dataset, we consistently outperform these existing scores. Specifically, we build simple classifiers that predict LLM response correctness by using these scores and the correctness of similar queries as features. Our method produces minimal computational overhead, making it a cheap and straightforward enhancement for UQ in LLMs for real-world applications.

</details>


### [135] [Mechanistic Tomography: Designed Measurement for Control-Oriented Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19338)
*Vijay Erramilli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"机理断层扫描"（mechanistic tomography）框架，将patching、梯度、Hessian-向量积和子集干预等机制可解释性方法统一为y = Ax + w的测量结构，并提供了一套从低成本测量到扩展测量族的实用流程，在多个模型（two-HMM、Tracr、GPT-2-small、Qwen-2.5-7B）上验证了该框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性领域缺乏统一的测量框架。现有方法（patching、梯度、Hessian-向量积等）在不同访问假设下测量不同目标量，难以比较和选择，也没有系统性的流程来迭代改进测量方案。

Method: 将不同的可解释性测量统一为y = Ax + w的线性测量模型，其中A描述干预方式，x为目标映射，w包含非线性响应、采样误差和基不匹配。提出实用流程：从最经济的测量开始，在保留干预上测试，校准简单失配，当存在结构化残差时扩展测量族。该框架将控制问题作为验证场景，在多个模型上系统比较不同测量族的效果。

Result: 在two-HMM模型中，控制误差随观测器误差上升，目标改进可能掩盖扰动状态移动；前向访问下稀疏聚合测量比坐标patching更高效；梯度访问可改进局部归因图；提升测量和Hessian-向量积可恢复一阶映射遗漏的交互；GPT-2-small IOI上Name Mover与Negative Name Mover交互是最大的跨组预测项；Qwen-2.5-7B上有限校准使加性拒绝响应图足够充分。

Conclusion: 机理断层扫描提供了一个统一且实用的测量框架，帮助研究者根据访问条件和任务目标选择适当的测量族，并通过迭代校准流程不断改进对模型内部机制的估计。框架的价值在于给出了从测量假设到结论的完整方法论，同时指出了不同基和测量族在不同模型上的适用性差异。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability seeks quantities that models do not expose directly: represented states, component effects, interactions, and responses to interventions. Patching, gradients, Hessian-vector products, and subset interventions provide different measurements under different access assumptions and may target different quantities. We formulate their shared measurement structure as mechanistic tomography: designed measurement for recovering internal mechanisms and intervention effects.
  For a chosen basis and intervention family, measurements take the form y = Ax + w, where A describes the interventions, x is the target map, and w contains nonlinear response, sampling error, and basis misspecification. This language gives a practical procedure: start with the least costly measurements, test on held-out interventions at the intended scale, calibrate simple mismatch, and expand the measurement family when structured residuals remain.
  Control provides a demanding validation setting because an estimate that guides an intervention acts as an observer. In a two-HMM model, control error rises with observer error, while target improvement can hide nuisance-state movement. Under forward-only access, sparse aggregate measurements recover a finite-effect map with fewer interventions than coordinate patching. With gradient access, finite probes improve a local attribution map. Lifted measurements and Hessian-vector products recover interactions missed by first-order maps, while Tracr shows that the required family depends on the basis. On GPT-2-small IOI, the Name Mover-Negative Name Mover interaction is the largest held-out predictive term among three tested cross-group pairs. On Qwen-2.5-7B, finite calibration makes an additive refusal-response map adequate, so held-out error does not support pairwise lifting.

</details>


### [136] [Uncovering the Limits of Proof Sharing for Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19351)
*Kanak Das,Shubham Ugare,Bor-Yuh Evan Chang,Sasa Misailovic,Gagandeep Singh,Manu Sridharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了基于模板加速的不完备神经网络鲁棒性验证技术，提出联合稳定神经元指标来解释不同场景下模板包含率的变化，并设计了FastCert技术自动跨层分配模板以提升性能，在L0验证任务上实现了1.13倍的平均加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模板重用技术（通过复用中间层抽象状态或模板加速不完备验证）在部分场景下有效，但模板加速在不同网络架构、属性、数据集和训练方法下的鲁棒性/有效性尚不清楚，缺乏系统性理解其适用范围和局限性。

Method: 1) 对模板加速技术在不同场景下进行系统性研究，衡量模板包含率（subsumption rate）的变化；2) 提出联合稳定神经元（jointly stable neurons）这一新指标来解释模板加速效果差异的原因；3) 提出FastCert技术，自动跨神经网络层分发模板以提升性能影响，并在模板不太可能带来加速时完全放弃使用模板。

Result: 系统研究表明模板包含率在不同场景下差异很大；联合稳定神经元指标可以有效解释这种差异，揭示在某些情况下基于模板的技术几乎不可能带来任何加速。在大量基于覆盖设计的L0验证任务中，FastCert相比现有的模板重用技术实现了平均1.13倍的加速。

Conclusion: 模板加速并非在所有场景下都有效，需要根据具体场景判断其适用性；通过联合稳定神经元指标可以预测模板加速的潜力，FastCert通过自动化的模板分配和取舍策略能够更有效地利用模板加速，整体上提升不完备验证的效率。

Abstract: Robustness verification of neural networks is increasingly important, due to their use in many critical domains. In certain scenarios, proof sharing has been shown to accelerate incomplete verification techniques by reusing intermediate-layer abstract states, or templates, across queries. However, questions remain as to the robustness of template-based acceleration across varying network architectures, properties, datasets, and training methods. In this work, we perform a systematic study of the effectiveness of template-based acceleration and its limits. Our study shows that template subsumption rates can vary widely across scenarios. We present a novel metric of jointly stable neurons to explain this variation, showing that in some cases template-based techniques are very unlikely to provide any speedup. Then, we present FastCert, a novel technique for automatically distributing templates across neural network layers to increase performance impact, eschewing templates entirely if they are unlikely to produce a speedup. Across a large set of covering-design based $L_0$-verification tasks, FastCert achieved an average speedup of 1.13x over an extant template-based reuse technique.

</details>


### [137] [Longitudinal Bayesian Learning of Continuous Disease Position across the Alzheimer's Disease Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19436)
*Yingying Zhang,Kun Zhao,Guodong Liu,Qi Huang,Pengfei Gu,Dongchul Kim,Erik Enriquez,Alex D. Leow,Paul M. Thompson,Heng Huang,Hongchang Gao,Liang Zhan,Haoteng Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出疾病连续体定位（DCP），一种纵向贝叶斯学习框架，从纵向扩散张量成像（DTI）中连续估计阿尔茨海默病（AD）的疾病严重程度，通过低维概率潜变量与弱临床监督的结合，推导出疾病连续体评分（DCS）来量化个体在AD连续体上的位置及其不确定性。在ADNI队列上的实验表明DCP优于代表性方法，DCS能准确刻画疾病严重程度、保持临床相关性、保留纵向演化并预测未来疾病转化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病本质上是一个连续的生物学过程，而现有基于神经影像的AI方法大多局限于横断面影像的离散诊断或临床评分预测，无法捕捉疾病的连续进展特性，缺乏对疾病严重程度的连续定量刻画。

Method: 提出DCP框架，将疾病严重程度建模为一种低维概率潜变量，通过联合整合纵向观测数据与弱临床监督进行学习，并从该潜变量推导出疾病连续体评分（DCS），同时提供相应的不确定性估计。

Result: 在ADNI队列上的大量实验表明，DCP持续优于代表性的疾病进展方法。综合验证分析显示DCS能准确表征疾病严重程度，具有强临床相关性，保留纵向疾病演化信息，并能预测未来的疾病转化。

Conclusion: DCS提供了超越传统诊断标签和临床评分的定量影像衍生表征，可用于阿尔茨海默病进展的连续评估，为疾病连续监测提供了新的影像学工具。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) progresses as a continuous biological process, whereas most existing neuroimaging-based artificial intelligence methods remain limited to discrete diagnosis or clinical score prediction from cross-sectional imaging. In this work, we propose Disease Continuum Positioning (DCP), a longitudinal Bayesian Learning framework that continuously estimates disease severity from longitudinal diffusion tensor imaging (DTI). Specifically, DCP models disease severity as a low-dimensional probabilistic latent variable by jointly integrating longitudinal observations with weak clinical supervision, from which the proposed Disease Continuum Score (DCS) is derived to quantify an individual's position along the Alzheimer's disease continuum together with its associated uncertainty. Extensive experiments on the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI) cohort demonstrate that DCP consistently outperforms representative disease progression methods. More importantly, comprehensive validation analyses show that DCS accurately characterizes disease severity, exhibits strong clinical relevance, preserves longitudinal disease evolution, and predicts future disease conversion. These results suggest that DCS provides a quantitative imaging-derived representation for continuous assessment of Alzheimer's disease progression beyond conventional diagnostic labels and clinical scores.

</details>


### [138] [Quantifying Event Impacts on Time Series via Multiscale Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19447)
*Yiming Sun,Shengyu Chen,Zhengzhang Chen,Haoyu Wang,Xiaowei Jia,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EventTime框架，用于预测网络安全事件披露后的短期金融市场异常损失，通过多分辨率建模（长期市场背景、短期事件前动态和事件元数据）结合事件融合模块和动态对比学习目标，缓解事件数据稀疏和异质性问题，并在新构建的SECURE数据集上验证其超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全事件（如网络攻击披露）会突发性地扰乱金融时间序列并造成异常损失，但现有时间序列预测模型主要关注内生规律（趋势、季节性、自相关），难以处理罕见且异质的外部事件。同时，高影响事件稀疏且背景市场噪声干扰大，使得事件条件化影响预测极具挑战性。

Method: EventTime是一个多分辨率框架，整合三个维度：长期市场背景、短期事件前动态和事件元数据。核心组件包括：(1)事件融合模块，将时间表示与事件属性耦合以识别相关近期市场模式；(2)动态对比学习目标，构造事件感知和时间序列感知的正负样本对，缓解稀疏监督问题；同时构建了SECURE真实世界数据集，将网络安全事件与股票市场时间序列及结构化、LLM推导的语义特征对齐。

Result: 在SECURE数据集上，EventTime在估计事件后财务损失方面持续优于最先进的时间序列和事件感知基线。进一步分析表明其具有更事件敏感的表征、对不完整元数据更强的鲁棒性，以及对网络安全披露后短期市场影响更可解释的估计。

Conclusion: EventTime有效解决了事件条件化影响预测问题，证明了融合事件元数据与多分辨率时间信息、并引入动态对比学习的价值，为网络安全事件对金融市场影响的可解释量化估计提供了新方法。

Abstract: Shocks that spread through the web, such as cybersecurity breach disclosures, can abruptly disrupt financial time series and cause substantial abnormal losses. While these events are disclosed as discrete records through news reports, regulatory filings, or public databases, their consequences unfold through continuous market dynamics. This creates an event-conditioned impact prediction problem: given pre-event market history and limited event metadata, the goal is to estimate short-term post-disclosure abnormal loss rather than reconstruct the full post-event trajectory. However, most time-series forecasting models focus on endogenous regularities such as trend, seasonality, and autocorrelation, and thus struggle with rare and heterogeneous external events. The challenge is further amplified by sparse high-impact events and background market noise. We introduce EventTime, a multi-resolution framework that combines long-horizon market context, short-horizon pre-event dynamics, and event metadata. It incorporates an event fusion module that couples temporal representations with event attributes to identify relevant recent market patterns. To mitigate sparse supervision, EventTime further introduces a dynamic contrastive objective that constructs event- and time-series-aware positive and negative pairs during training. We also construct SECURE, a real-world dataset aligning cybersecurity incidents with stock-market time series and structured and LLM-derived semantic features. Experiments show that EventTime consistently outperforms state-of-the-art time-series and event-aware baselines in estimating post-event financial losses. Further analyses demonstrate more event-sensitive representations, greater robustness to incomplete metadata, and more interpretable estimates of short-term market impact following cybersecurity disclosures.

</details>


### [139] [LLM as Detector: An In-context Learning Approach for Tabular Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19463)
*Tu Anh Hoang Nguyen,Dang Nguyen,Thuc Duy Le,Trung Le,Sunil Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LLM-Detector框架，利用大语言模型的上下文学习能力，通过将正常训练数据转化为统计摘要、因果依赖和提炼原型并组织成提示词来生成评分代码，实现表格数据的异常检测，无需微调或神经网络训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据中的异常样本往往体现为跨特征依赖关系的违反，而非简单的边缘偏差。现有检测器依赖几何或重建信号，而先前基于LLM的方法主要对LLM进行微调或生成合成异常，缺乏利用LLM上下文学习能力直接从结构化正常状态知识中推导异常检测逻辑的方法。

Method: 将正常训练数据转换为三种结构化知识：统计摘要、因果依赖和提炼原型，组织成提示词供LLM生成评分代码。生成的评分引擎综合评估统计偏差、结构不一致性和基于密度的异常，为每个测试样本计算异常分数。

Result: 在24个表格数据集上与15个SOTA基线进行比较，在混合类型和连续型设置下均表现出一致的改进。

Conclusion: 该设计无需LLM微调或神经网络训练，降低了计算成本，使表格系统能够实现实用的异常检测。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in tabular data is challenging because abnormal samples often arise as violations of cross-feature dependencies rather than simple marginal deviations. Existing detectors rely on geometric or reconstruction signals, while prior LLM-based approaches mainly fine-tune LLMs with normal samples or generate synthetic anomalies. We propose LLM-Detector, a framework that utilizes the in-context learning capacity of LLMs for structured, prompt-conditioned scoring synthesis, enabling LLMs to derive anomaly detection logic from structured normal-state knowledge. Specifically, normal training data are converted into statistical summaries, causal dependencies, and distilled prototypes that are organized into a prompt for code generation. The resulting scoring engine evaluates statistical deviation, structural inconsistency, and density-based abnormality then computes an anomaly score for each test sample. We evaluate LLM-Detector on 24 tabular datasets, comparing against 15 SOTA baselines. Results show consistent improvements across both mixed-type and continuous-only settings. Moreover, this design eliminates the need for LLM fine-tuning or neural network training, reducing computational cost and enabling practical anomaly detection in real-world tabular systems.

</details>


### [140] [When to Retrain: An Empirical Study of Retraining Policies for Streaming ML Under Concept Drift, Budget, and Latency Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19488)
*Sawan Dasari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过3,933次实验，系统研究了生产机器学习系统在概念漂移下的模型刷新策略。核心发现是：最具影响力的设计决策不是重训练策略本身，而是部署模型是否支持增量学习。在支持增量学习且标签即时可得的线性在线学习器上，所有策略与不重训练基线均无显著差异；而在无增量学习时，周期性重训练显著优于响应式策略（除了循环漂移场景）。研究还揭示了响应式策略的失败模式及延迟-预算队列交互问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产机器学习系统在概念漂移下性能会退化，但实践者缺乏关于何时重训练的可靠指导。重训练成本高昂、预算有限，且训练和部署延迟会导致陈旧模型在数据持续变化时继续服务。现有研究缺乏在统一系统模型下对重训练策略的系统性比较。

Method: 构建统一系统模型（显式建模重训练预算和训练+部署延迟），对三种实际模型刷新策略（周期性重训练、误差阈值触发、基于ADWIN的统计漂移触发）与不重训练基线进行受控实证研究。实验覆盖3个漂移机制、3个预算水平、最多5个延迟级别、3个数据集和2种学习模式，共3,933次实验。

Result: 1) 增量学习使所有策略与基线无实际显著差异（54组成对比较）；2) 无增量学习时，策略选择造成15-55个百分点的漂移后准确率差异，且周期性重训练在突变和渐变漂移下显著优于两种响应式策略，响应式策略仅在循环漂移下保留优势；3) 记录了响应式策略的系统性失败模式和延迟-预算队列交互（会静默减半有效重训练预算）。

Conclusion: 模型刷新策略的选择重要性远低于部署模型是否支持增量学习。在无增量学习场景下，简单的周期性重训练是更稳健的选择。研究结果对实践者提供指导意义，并开源了完整模拟器、数据集流水线和逐次运行产物以保证可复现性。

Abstract: Production machine learning systems degrade under concept drift, yet practitioners have little principled guidance on when to retrain. Retraining is costly, retraining budgets are finite, and a retrained model does not take effect instantly: training and deployment latency leave a stale model serving predictions while the data continues to move. We present a controlled empirical study of three practical model-refresh policies (periodic retraining, error-threshold triggering, and statistical drift-triggered retraining with ADWIN) against a no-retrain baseline, evaluated under a unified system model that makes retraining budgets and training-plus-deployment latency explicit. Across 3,933 experiment runs spanning three drift regimes, three budget levels, up to five latency levels, three datasets, and two learning modes, we find that the single most consequential design decision is not the retraining policy but whether the deployed model learns incrementally. With per-sample incremental updates, and for the linear online learner with immediate labels studied here, no policy differs from the no-retrain baseline by a practically significant margin in any of 54 paired comparisons, even at extreme latency. Without incremental updates, policy choice separates outcomes by 15-55 percentage points of post-drift accuracy, and simple periodic retraining significantly outperforms both reactive policies under abrupt and gradual drift, while reactive policies retain an advantage only under recurring drift. We document systematic failure modes of reactive policies and a latency-budget queueing interaction that silently halves effective retraining budgets, and release the full simulator, dataset pipelines, and per-run artifacts for reproducibility.

</details>


### [141] [DeltaMomentum: A Key-Value based Anisotropic Momentum Update via Delta Rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19491)
*Euijin Hong,Guannan Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DeltaMomentum，一种方向感知的动量更新规则。与传统EMA动量以固定速率遗忘所有方向不同，DeltaMomentum利用线性层梯度的键值结构（输入作为key、输出侧误差作为value），通过delta规则更新动量缓冲，使每个方向按出现频率被遗忘。它可作为任何优化器的即插即用动量缓冲替代，在预训练中显著加速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统优化器（如AdamW）的动量采用EMA形式，以固定速率遗忘所有方向。但深度网络训练输入具有高度各向异性，少数方向频繁出现而大多数方向罕见。现有方法通过在动量缓冲外包装额外处理来解决此问题，但未改动动量更新本身，因此作者希望将方向感知直接构建到动量更新规则中。

Method: 1) 观察：线性层梯度可分解为输入（key）和输出侧误差（value）；2) 利用键值结构，用规范的delta规则更新动量缓冲，使各方向遗忘率根据出现频率自适应；3) 证明其是有效动量，可应用输入侧曲率校正且无需矩阵求逆，在固定/漂移最优条件下比EMA更快清除陈旧方向；4) 作为任何优化器的即插即用动量缓冲替代，系数在μP下跨宽度可迁移，额外计算量仅为gated-MLP模块线性成本的22.2%-25.0%，无需持久内存。

Result: 在FineWeb-Edu预训练中，DeltaAdamW相比AdamW达到相同验证损失最多节省46.39±4.32%（67M）和22.12±0.80%（370M）训练步数（三种子），在1B规模Chinchilla最优预算下增益保持。在语言模型尺度上优于Muon基线，对SGD、ResNet-18、ViT-Tiny在CIFAR-10上同样有效。训练时诊断确认了预测机制：更好的梯度跟踪和更健康的输入方向。

Conclusion: DeltaMomentum通过将方向感知融入动量更新规则本身，有效解决了训练输入各向异性问题。它实现了显著的训练加速，具有良好的通用性和即插即用特性，且计算开销可控，为优化器设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Most modern optimizers form their momentum as an exponential moving average (EMA) of past gradients, forgetting every direction at one fixed rate. However, the inputs a deep network sees during training can be highly anisotropic, with a few directions queried frequently while most are seen rarely. Recent methods address this anisotropy by wrapping extra processing around this buffer, leaving the momentum update itself unchanged. We propose DeltaMomentum, which builds direction-awareness into the momentum update rule. The main observation is that the gradient of a linear layer splits into an input that acts as a key and an output-side error that acts as a value. Exploiting the key-value structure, DeltaMomentum updates the momentum buffer by the canonical delta rule, so each direction is forgotten at a rate set by how often it appears. We prove that it is a valid momentum, that it applies the input-side curvature correction without matrix inversion, and that it clears stale directions faster than EMA under both a fixed and a drifting optimum. It is a drop-in replacement for the momentum buffer of any optimizer, its coefficient transfers across widths under $μ$P, and its extra compute stays between $22.2\%$ and $25.0\%$ of a gated-MLP block's linear cost with no persistent memory. In FineWeb-Edu pretraining, AdamW with DeltaMomentum (DeltaAdamW) reaches AdamW's validation loss in up to $46.39 \pm 4.32\%$ fewer steps at 67M and $22.12 \pm 0.80\%$ at 370M over three seeds, and the gain persists at 1B on a Chinchilla-optimal budget. A Muon baseline tuned under the same protocol sits above DeltaAdamW at both language-model scales, and the gain holds for SGD, ResNet-18, and ViT-Tiny on CIFAR-10. Training-time diagnostics confirm the predicted mechanism, better gradient tracking and healthier input directions.

</details>


### [142] [Beyond Multimodal Alignment: Certifying Physical Language through Response Substitution and Ordered Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19492)
*Kaizhen Tan,Xin Xu,Siru Tao,Yixiao Li,Hanzhe Hong,Yang Feng,Heqing Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种操作性能力层级和不相交桥算子替换证书（DBOSC），用于检验世界模型中不同模态编译器的可执行语义是否可互换，以及该语义能否在新动作组合下存活。实验将属性访问、响应替换、融合闭包和有序执行区分为独立可测试的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有探测方法无法确定不同传感器是否承载相同的可执行语义，也无法验证该语义在新型动作组合下是否依然有效。这阻碍了对世界模型多模态表示作为感知与物理交互接口的可靠评估。

Method: 提出操作能力层级（operational capability hierarchy）和DBOSC证书，检验独立训练的多模态编译器能否在冻结响应图上、基于训练面板之外的证据进行互换。在Cluster Haptic数据集上测试模态表示的空间接近度，并在受控弹塑性系统中测试有序执行与融合闭包，包括预注册预算和收敛预算两种条件下的门控检验。

Result: 在Cluster Haptic上，音频和加速度表示在响应空间中比错误表面配对接近4.5倍，19个留出表面全部保持。融合改进优于单一模态（NMSE 0.18），14/16项注册检查通过；两项失败源于融合信息矩阵的对角限制与完整矩阵相当。发射完整程序的执行器比实体无关预测器差38倍。收敛预算下排名三的图表可执行程序，而预注册预算下不可执行。

Conclusion: 通过门控是执行器而非图表的属性；压缩和融合本身无法确定未见组合律（非可辨识性结果）。文章成功将属性访问、响应替换、融合闭包和有序执行区分为四种独立且可分别测试的能力成就。

Abstract: World models increasingly treat compact multimodal representations as interfaces between perception and physical interaction, yet existing probes do not establish whether different sensors carry the same executable meaning or whether that meaning survives a new action composition. We introduce an operational capability hierarchy and the Disjoint-Bridge Operator-Substitution Certificate (DBOSC), which asks whether independently trained modality compilers enter a frozen response chart interchangeably on evidence outside their training panels. On Cluster Haptic, audio and acceleration representations of the same unseen surface are 4.5x closer in response space than wrong-surface pairings, with the gap holding for all 19 held-out surfaces; unsealing withheld responses confirms that every branch predicts the physics better than the population chart. We then test ordered execution in a controlled elastoplastic system with complementary modality blind spots. At the pre-registered budget, the prerequisite refuses the stack because the frozen executor cannot advance even an exact chart coordinate through a held-out program. At a converged budget, the same rank-three chart executes those programs (oracle NMSE 0.18), fusion improves on both modalities, and 14 of 16 registered checks pass; the two failures arise because a diagonal restriction of the fused information matrix performs as well as the full one. Clearing the gate is a property of the executor, not the chart: an executor emitting whole programs instead of shared per-step dynamics is 38x worse than an entity-blind predictor on the same chart. A matching non-identifiability result explains why compression and fusion alone cannot determine an unseen composition law. These results separate attribute access, response substitution, fusion closure, and ordered execution into distinct, separately testable achievements.

</details>


### [143] [Empirical Characterization of Learning Geometry in Hybrid Quantum Forecasting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19497)
*Sandra Leticia Juárez-Osorio,Jorge I. Hernandez-Martinez,Jesus Ivan Ruiz-Martinez,Andres Mendez-Vazquez,Eduardo Rodriguez-Tello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过对比紧凑混合量子预测模型与结构对齐的经典基线模型，利用核目标对齐、核漂移、谱集中度和训练损失等NTK动力学指标，系统分析了两者在优化几何上的差异，发现尽管学习轨迹显著不同，两者在多种频率条件下仍能达到相当的性能水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究往往仅以端到端精度来衡量量子模型的优势，忽视了学习动力学和优化几何层面的差异。作者希望通过NTK视角揭示量子与经典模型在训练过程中表现出的架构依赖性行为差异，从而为理解量子模型的学习特性提供更深入的依据，避免仅凭最终精度掩盖潜在的结构性区别。

Method: 使用平稳谐波混合和非平稳啁啾两种基准任务，控制谱复杂度和数据可用性。通过经验神经正切核（NTK）分析，计算核目标对齐、核漂移、谱集中度及训练损失。对比紧凑混合量子模型（125个可训练参数）与结构对齐的经典基线（281个可训练参数），并设置傅里叶增强经典基线和可控数据重上传消融实验。

Result: 经典模型早期目标对齐更强，而混合量子模型通常具有更不集中的核谱和更小的核漂移。尽管优化几何不同，两者在18个频率条件中的15个中，混合量子模型更早达到验证选择检查点，且实现了相当的性能。傅里叶增强的经典基线无法复现观测到的训练行为，重上传消融表明重复编码系统性地改变了优化和核几何。

Conclusion: 可比的泛化性能可以从显著不同的学习轨迹中产生，单一的NTK诊断指标不能作为验证收敛的单调预测器。研究不主张普遍的量子优势，而是指出仅凭最终精度会掩盖架构依赖的学习行为差异。

Abstract: We characterize the learning dynamics of a compact hybrid quantum forecasting model through comparison with a structurally aligned classical baseline. Using stationary harmonic-mixture and nonstationary chirp benchmarks with controlled spectral complexity and data availability, we analyze empirical Neural Tangent Kernel dynamics through kernel-target alignment, kernel drift, spectral concentration, and training loss. The classical model exhibits stronger early target alignment, whereas the hybrid model generally develops a less concentrated kernel spectrum and smaller kernel drift. Despite these distinct optimization geometries, both architectures attain similar held-out performance across the evaluated regimes. Notably, the hybrid model uses 125 trainable parameters compared with 281 for the classical baseline and reaches its validation-selected checkpoint earlier in 15 of 18 frequency conditions. A Fourier-augmented classical baseline does not reproduce the observed training behavior, while a controlled re-uploading ablation shows that repeated encoding systematically modifies both optimization and kernel geometry. These results demonstrate that comparable generalization can emerge from substantially different learning trajectories and that individual NTK diagnostics do not provide monotonic predictors of validation convergence. Rather than claiming a general quantum advantage, the study identifies architecture-dependent learning behavior that is masked by endpoint accuracy alone.

</details>


### [144] [In Two Minds about Lifelong Learning: Exploring Hemispheric Redundancy and Specialisation in Neural Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19514)
*Benjamin Smith,Levin Kuhlmann,Kaushik Roy,Gideon Kowadlo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出4MAS（4模块清醒/睡眠）宏观架构，通过模拟动物的经验回放、REM睡眠和大脑双侧性，解决机器学习中的灾难性遗忘问题。该架构采用不对称半球各自配备长短时记忆机制，并在增量学习任务之间引入睡眠期以促进记忆巩固，在Split-MNIST（98.3%）、Split-Fashion-MNIST（84.9%）和Split-CIFAR-100（29.29%）上取得有竞争力的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续智能系统需要持续学习能力，但当前机器学习方法在保留旧知识和适应新数据之间面临权衡。当缺乏持续学习能力时，算法必须用全部数据重新训练，而原始数据可能因存储限制、计算成本或隐私限制无法获得。相比之下，生物系统能持续学习而不发生灾难性遗忘，因此需要从生物学中获取灵感构建持续学习框架。

Method: 本文构建了一个基于生物记忆巩固机制的高层框架，关注三个核心概念：经验回放（experience replay）、REM睡眠和双侧性（bilaterality），并提出4MAS宏观架构。该架构包含不对称的半球，每个半球拥有自己的长期和短期记忆机制，并在增量学习任务之间设置睡眠阶段以促进记忆巩固。

Result: 4MAS架构在三个基准数据集上取得有竞争力结果：Split-MNIST准确率98.3%，Split-Fashion-MNIST准确率84.9%，Split-CIFAR-100准确率29.29%。

Conclusion: 研究表明，借鉴生物学习系统的记忆巩固机制（特别是不对称半球结构和睡眠的作用）可以为机器学习提供有效的持续学习方案，4MAS证明了这种生物启发式宏观架构的可行性，为后续持续学习研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Persistent intelligent systems require the ability to learn continually, but current machine learning approaches face significant challenges in this area compared to biological learning systems. Machine learning algorithms typically trade off retention of previously learned information and adaptation to new or changing data patterns. When continual learning capabilities are absent, algorithms must undergo retraining using the entire data set, an approach that becomes impractical when original training data are unavailable due to storage constraints, financial or computational costs, or privacy restrictions. However, biological animals can learn continually, without experiencing catastrophic forgetting. This paper attempts to build a high-level framework for how animals learn and preserve knowledge by modelling neural components and states that are known to be related to memory consolidation. We focus on three concepts: experience replay, REM sleep, and bilaterality. We propose 4MAS (4 Module Awake/Sleep), a novel macroarchitecture demonstrating how machine learning models might benefit from asymmetric hemispheres, each with their own long- and short-term memory mechanisms, and how a period of sleep between incremental learning tasks might benefit memory consolidation. Finally, we present results showing that our architecture achieves competitive results on the Split-MNIST, Split-Fashion-MNIST and Split-CIFAR-100 datasets, with 98.3%, 84.9%, and 29.29% accuracy respectively.

</details>


### [145] [Continuous Adversarial MeanFlow Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19540)
*Yara Bahram,Zahra Dehghani,Mélodie Desbos,Eric Granger,Pablo Piantanida,Mohammadhadi Shateri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MeanFlow-Transfer (MF-T)和Continuous Adversarial MeanFlow (CAMF)两个方法，将异构预训练扩散/流模型映射到统一的平均速度表示，实现少步快速生成，并在新域上通过连续对抗训练恢复细节，在FID/FDD上与微调教师模型相当甚至更优，同时减少高达125倍的NFE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在新领域用有限数据训练快速生成器面临两个核心挑战：一是将预训练扩散/流模型适配到新域时，现有的加速方法依赖于源模型的参数化（ε、x、v或u），异构模型缺乏统一的加速目标；二是对抗性精炼仅针对瞬时速度流模型设计，无法适用于MeanFlow (MF)模型预测的有限区间平均速度。

Method: 提出两个组件：(1) MeanFlow-Transfer (MF-T)：将异构源模型的输出映射到共享的平均速度表示，用源权重初始化MF生成器，并在目标域上优化MF目标，在单一训练循环中统一适配和加速；(2) Continuous Adversarial MeanFlow (CAMF)：后训练阶段，将连续对抗流模型从瞬时速度扩展到MF的有限区间平均速度，通过对比真实与预测区间端点间学习势的变化来恢复被MF回归平均掉的细节，在区间趋近于零时退化为瞬时准则。

Result: 将四种基于ImageNet的源模型（DiT-ε、SiT-v、JiT-x、iMF-u）适配到五个目标域，MF-T结合CAMF在FID和FDD指标上达到或超过微调教师模型，同时NFE减少高达125倍；CAMF平均将MF-T的少步FID提升29%。

Conclusion: MF-T和CAMF共同解决了跨参数化的生成模型适配与加速的统一问题，证明有限区间平均速度表示可以作为一个通用的加速目标，且连续对抗训练能有效恢复MF回归丢失的细节，在不同预训练模型族上均展现出强泛化能力。

Abstract: Training fast generators on new domains with limited data remains challenging for two reasons. First, adapting a pretrained diffusion or flow model to a new domain leaves its costly multi-step sampling unaddressed, and existing acceleration methods are tied to the source parameterization--$ε$, $x$, $v$, or $u$--leaving heterogeneous pretrained models with no common acceleration target. Second, while adversarial refinement is proven effective for few-step quality, it is formulated only for instantaneous-velocity flows, not for the finite-interval average velocities that MeanFlow (MF) models predict. We address both problems. We propose MeanFlow-Transfer, which maps heterogeneous source outputs into a shared velocity representation, uses it to initialize an MF generator from the source weights, and optimizes an MF objective on the target domain. This unifies adaptation and acceleration in a single training loop across a broad range of pretrained models. We then introduce Continuous Adversarial MeanFlow, a post-training stage that extends continuous adversarial flow models from instantaneous velocities to MF's finite-interval average velocities. CAMF contrasts changes in a learned potential between real and predicted interval endpoints, recovering fine detail that MF regression averages away, and reduces to the instantaneous criterion in the vanishing-interval limit. Adapting four ImageNet-based source models--DiT ($ε$), SiT ($v$), JiT ($x$), iMF ($u$)--to five target domains, MF-T with CAMF matches or exceeds the fine-tuned teacher in FID and FDD at up to $125\times$ fewer Neural Function Evaluations (NFEs), while CAMF improves MF-T's few-step FID by $29\%$ on average.

</details>


### [146] [DraftFM: A FoundationModel for Day-Zero Drafting in Magic: The Gathering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19568)
*Brian Ward*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DraftFM 是一个离散选择模型，专门用于"第零天"（day-zero）环境下的万智牌轮抽预测。它只用公开的卡牌记录（775维结构化特征加固定文本嵌入）给当前卡包中的卡牌打分，完全不含卡牌身份、系列身份或使用统计信息，因此对未见过的卡牌同样适用。模型仅160万参数，在1.49亿人类轮抽选择（29个系列）上训练，对3个完全保留的系列达到50.8%、60.4%、56.7%的top-1准确率（均匀随机基线约7%）。研究人员将所有32个已观察系列重训后，对未发布的"哈比人"系列生成了卡牌排名，密封并提前约36小时公开，该排名与六位独立专家评审之间的一致性大致相当于专家彼此之间的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的轮抽预测模型依赖已有轮抽日志进行监督训练，但新系列发布时（day-zero阶段）尚无任何人类轮抽记录可供训练。作者希望在不依赖卡牌身份、使用统计等"后验"信息的条件下，仅凭公开的卡牌文本和结构特征，让模型能够对完全未见过的卡牌做出合理的轮抽决策，从而提前预测新系列的卡牌价值。

Method: DraftFM 是一个离散选择策略（discrete-choice policy），在当前卡包内逐张精确计分，以已抓牌库和轮抽状态为条件。每张卡以冻结的775维表示进入模型（来自公开卡牌记录、结构化特征和固定文本嵌入的组合），模型不含卡牌身份、系列身份或使用统计。模型仅160万参数，在1.49亿个人类轮抽选择（来自29个系列）上训练。对于部分测试，用全部32个已观察系列重训同一架构，为未发布的"哈比人"系列生成密封卡牌排名（带完整加密来源证明），提前约36小时公开。

Result: 对三个完全保留（不参与训练）系列的held-out预测top-1准确率分别为50.8%、60.4%、56.7%，而第一抓均匀随机基线约7%。为未发布"哈比人"系列生成的密封排名与六位独立专家评审的一致性，大约相当于这六位专家彼此之间的一致性水平。对实际轮抽结果的评估留待后续笔记公布。

Conclusion: DraftFM 证明了一个完全不依赖卡牌身份、系列归属或使用统计的纯"公共信息"模型，能够在day-zero（新系列无训练数据）情境下以远超随机基线的精度预测人类轮抽偏好，其预测水平接近人类专家之间的共识程度。该工作为未发布内容的价值预测提供了一种可验证、带密码学来源的评估框架。

Abstract: Drafting a new Magic: The Gathering expansion begins before any pick from it has been observed: the complete card list is public, but the draft logs that supervised pick models train on do not yet exist. We study this day-zero regime directly. DraftFM is a discrete-choice policy that scores exactly the cards available in the current pack, conditioned on the drafted pool and the state of the draft. Every card enters as a frozen 775-dimensional function of its public card record, structured features and a fixed text embedding, with no card identities, set identities, or usage statistics anywhere in the model, so an unseen card is scored by the same machinery as a familiar one. A 1.6-million-parameter network fitted on 149 million human picks from 29 expansions predicts held-out picks in three expansions withheld in their entirety, reaching 50.8%, 60.4%, and 56.7% top-1 agreement, where uniform chance at the opening pick is about 7%. Refitted on all 32 observed expansions, the same architecture produced a card ranking for the then-unreleased set The Hobbit, sealed with its complete cryptographic provenance and published roughly 36 hours before the set became draftable on MTG Arena. The sealed ranking agrees with six independent expert reviewers roughly as much as those reviewers agree with one another. Evaluation against realized outcomes is committed to a follow-on note, whatever it shows.

</details>


### [147] [A Two-Stage Time-Aware Transformer for Short-Horizon AECOPD Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19578)
*Dongyang Wang,Weihao Qu,Ling Zheng,Haowen Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段深度学习框架，直接处理家用呼吸机最近七天的原始压力和流量波形数据。第一阶段分类模型识别高危慢阻肺急性加重（AECOPD）患者，第二阶段回归模型预测距离事件发生的天数。实验结果显示分类F1=0.91，回归RMSE=1.00天、R²=0.76，为临床提供早期预警和可操作的提前量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢阻肺急性加重（AECOPD）恶化迅速，及时预测是临床优先事项。现有机器学习方法依赖间断采集的临床变量，存在延迟，难以用于家庭监测场景；而家用呼吸机虽能提供近连续的低延迟呼吸数据，但现有基于呼吸机的方法要么压缩波形为手工特征，要么仅做二元风险分类，无法解决事件发生时机的问题。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段分类模型基于最近七天家用呼吸机的原始压力和流量波形识别高风险患者；第二阶段回归模型估计距离严重加重发生的剩余天数。模型直接操作原始波形数据，无需手工特征工程。

Result: 两阶段模型在风险分类和时间-事件估计任务上均优于传统基线模型。Stage 1分类器F1=0.91，Stage 2回归模型RMSE=1.00天、R²=0.76。

Conclusion: 该两阶段框架能够同时提供早期预警（识别高风险患者）和可操作的提前量（预测剩余天数），有助于临床医生在严重加重发生前提前干预，提升家庭监测场景中AECOPD预测的实用性和时效性。

Abstract: Acute exacerbation of chronic obstructive pulmonary disease (AECOPD) can worsen rapidly, making timely prediction a clinical priority. Most existing machine learning approaches rely on episodically collected clinical variables, introducing delays that limit their practical utility in home monitoring settings. Home ventilators offer a lower-latency alternative, producing a near-continuous record of respiratory status during daily use.
