Method: 提出基于正交预测分解的潜在世界模型框架：1) 学习基矩阵将目标状态分解为多个组件；2) 每个组件由共享上下文表示通过专用预测分支估算；3) 采用预测回归保持因子幅值、正交性目标避免重复方向、因子活动正则化维持投影目标变化、在线方差正则化防止编码器逐坐标坍缩；4) 预测组件合成完整潜在状态用于读取、解码、规划或自回归 rollout。该机制适用于时间未来、空间隐藏或同一系统的其他部分观测。

Result: 在受控视觉、单细胞转录组学、纵向健康记录、连续控制和分子动力学等多样化实验上评估了表征质量、预测、规划以及长时程稳定性，验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过正交预测分解，该方法能够更有效地捕捉复杂系统中的多层次预测结构，克服了标准JEPA的容量分配和梯度冲突问题，为构建更强大的潜在世界模型提供了一种通用且可扩展的框架。

Abstract: World models construct latent states that support prediction, planning, and reasoning about an underlying system. Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) offer a direct way to learn such states by predicting targets in representation space instead of reconstructing every detail of the observation. Standard JEPAs, however, organize all predictable content through one target embedding and one prediction pathway. In complex systems, this monolithic state can allocate redundant capacity to dominant signals while providing weak or conflicting gradients to less dominant predictive structure. We introduce \method, a latent world-modeling framework based on orthogonal predictive factorization. Learned basis matrices analyze each target state into multiple components, and a dedicated prediction branch estimates each component from a shared context representation. Predictive regression preserves the factor magnitudes required for state synthesis, an orthogonality objective discourages repeated directions, factor-activity regularization maintains variation in projected targets, and online variance regularization discourages coordinate-wise encoder collapse. Predicted components are synthesized into a complete latent state that can be used by a readout, decoder, planner, or autoregressive rollout. The same predictive-state mechanism applies when the target is temporally future, spatially hidden, or another partial observation of the same system. Experiments on controlled vision, single-cell transcriptomics, longitudinal health records, continuous control, and molecular dynamics evaluate representation quality, forecasting, planning, and long-horizon stability.

</details>


### [183] [SAE-Xplainers: Rule-Based Feature Interpretation for Extreme Earth Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20117)
*Hugo Porta,Emanuele Dalsasso,Chang Xu,Theo Gnassounou,Devis Tuia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于地理位置的SAE输入调制方法以及规则驱动的SAE-Xplainers集成框架，用于解释极端地球事件预测中的深度模型。方法在火灾预测、热带气旋和大气河检测三个任务上验证，提升了重建性能、特征利用率，并能将复杂气候模式解释为符合科学文献的可理解规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模天气与气候数据集为深度学习建模极端地球事件提供了新机会，但缺乏模型可解释性限制了其在业务场景中的实际应用。传统文本和图像领域中的稀疏自编码器（SAE）虽能提取人类可理解的概念，但由于W&C数据的特殊性（多模态、地理空间性强），直接应用SAE到极端事件分析中仍存在挑战。

Method: (i) 提出基于地理位置的输入调制方法，对SAE输入进行调制以捕捉环境模式的地理局部语义含义；(ii) 构建基于规则的SAE-Xplainers集成框架，用于解释从复杂的多模态环境预测因子中提取的高维特征。

Result: 在三种极端事件类型（火灾预测、热带气旋检测、大气河检测）上的实验表明，SAE输入调制改善了重建性能和特征利用率；SAE-Xplainers能够将复杂的天气气候模式展开为符合科学文献的人类可理解规则，并支持识别特征吸收（feature absorption）现象。

Conclusion: 该方法有效提升了W&C领域极端事件深度模型的可解释性，验证了将SAE与基于规则的解释器结合用于气候模式分析的可行性，为深度学习在气象业务中的部署提供了可解释性支持。

Abstract: The emergence of large-scale Weather and Climate (W&C) datasets offers new opportunities for modeling extreme Earth events (ExEE) and their impacts using deep learning. However, their adoption in operational settings remains limited by the lack of models' interpretability. While for conventional text and image modalities, tools such as Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have proven effective for extracting human-understandable concepts, their use for the analysis of ExEE remains challenging due to the nature of W&C data. To address this, we introduce (i) a geographic location-based modulation of the inputs of SAE to capture the local semantic meaning of environmental patterns, and (ii) an ensemble of rule-based SAE-Xplainers to interpret the resulting high-dimensional features derived from complex, multi-modal environmental predictors. We evaluate our method on three ExEE types: the prediction of fires, and the detection of tropical cyclones and atmospheric rivers. We show that SAE input modulation improves both reconstruction performance and feature utilization, and that our SAE-Xplainers enable faithful interpretation of complex climatic patterns by unfolding them into human-understandable rules that are consistent with the scientific literature, while also supporting the identification of feature absorption.

</details>


### [184] [Evaluating Neural Cartographic Relief Shading for Urban Environments: A Downtown Calgary Study Using High-Resolution DEM and DSM Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20149)
*Emmanuel Stefanakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对比了分析式与基于神经网络的明暗地形渲染方法在密集城市环境中的表现，使用卡尔加里市中心的DEM和DSM数据，探讨为阿尔卑斯山地景观训练的机器学习系统Eduard能否通过参数调优在城市场景中产生具有制图价值的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Eduard等神经网络山体阴影系统专为自然山地景观训练，对建筑、桥梁、街道、树木等城市基础设施并不适用。作者希望探索该神经方法是否能通过参数调整适应高度建成环境，并明确其在哪些方面优于或劣于传统分析方法。

Method: 使用卡尔加里市中心的高分辨率DEM和DSM数据，对比单方向与多方向分析式山体阴影方法，以及Eduard机器学习系统生成的浮雕阴影。神经实验聚焦于地形类型、微观与宏观泛化、平坦区域细节等参数，同时保持大尺度阴影风格不变，采用探索性比较而非通用基准测试的框架。

Result: 研究识别出分析式山体阴影在哪些方面仍然更可靠，Eduard在哪些方面展现出意外优势，以及神经阴影因训练偏向高山地形而在哪些场景下失效。

Conclusion: 作者主张未来应针对城市浮雕阴影进行专门的模型训练与评估，而非直接迁移为自然地形设计的神经方法。

Abstract: This article explores the performance of analytical and neural-based hillshading methods in a dense urban environment using high-resolution digital elevation model (DEM) and digital surface model (DSM) data for downtown Calgary. The study compares single-direction and multi-direction analytical hillshading with relief shading generated in Eduard, a machine-learning system originally developed to emulate Swiss-style shaded relief trained primarily on mountainous landscapes. Because Eduard was not designed for buildings, bridges, streets, trees, and other urban infrastructures, the central question is not whether it perfectly reproduces urban morphology, but whether parameter tuning can nevertheless produce visually strong, cartographically useful, and in some cases superior results when compared with conventional analytical methods. The analysis focuses especially on terrain type, micro and macro generalization, and flat-area detail parameters, while keeping the large-scale shading style constant throughout the neural experiments. The article is structured as an exploratory comparison rather than a benchmark of universal best practice. It aims to identify where analytical hillshading remains more reliable, where Eduard offers unexpected strengths, and where neural shading fails because of its training bias toward alpine terrain. The study contributes to current work on terrain representation by testing whether a neural approach designed for natural landforms can be adapted to a highly built urban setting, and it concludes by arguing for future model training and evaluation specifically targeted at urban relief shading.

</details>


### [185] [Ask Self, Ask Others: Relation Is All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20172)
*Yuting Ge,Pengju Yang,Mingkai Nie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Relation，一种替代注意力机制（Attention）的token混合原语。与注意力直接由成对分数推导归一化信息流不同，Relation先将成对证据组织为显式的Self和Exchange关系，再据此推导信息流。这种关系优先的组织方式衍生出Full Relation、FlashRelation、Linear Relation、Hybrid Relation以及KV风格的Relation Cache等多种变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力机制直接从成对相似度分数推导归一化信息流，缺乏对token之间关系类型的显式建模。作者认为，先明确"自我"与"交换"关系，再让信息流动，可能更符合语言建模的本质需求，因此探索一种以关系为先的token混合范式。

Method: 引入Relation作为新的token混合原语，核心思想是将成对证据组织为Self（自我）与Exchange（交换）两类显式关系，然后基于这些关系推导信息流。论文提出多种实现变体：Full Relation（精确实现）、FlashRelation（内存高效实现，对标FlashAttention）、Linear Relation（线性复杂度版本）、Hybrid Relation（混合结构）以及KV风格的Relation Cache。

Result: 在约10M、30M和100M参数的decoder-only模型上，Full Relation在三个规模下均取得比MHA更低的最终验证NLL。在固定上下文参考基准中，FlashRelation比物化的Full Relation实现快3.60-4.41倍。在规模匹配的生产工作负载中，FlashRelation达到PyTorch FlashAttention吞吐量的76.4-84.9%。Hybrid Relation使用75%的Linear Relation层，仍保持较强的语言建模质量。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持"关系优先"（relation-first）的token混合观点：先询问自我（Self）、再询问他人（Others），最后让信息流遵循关系（Flow follow Relation）。Relation范式在质量与效率上均展现出与注意力机制竞争甚至超越的潜力。

Abstract: Attention directly derives normalized information flow from pairwise scores. We introduce Relation, an alternative token-mixing primitive that first organizes pairwise evidence into explicit Self and Exchange relations and derives information flow afterward. This relational organization gives rise to Full Relation, FlashRelation, Linear Relation, Hybrid Relation, and a KV-style Relation Cache. Across matched decoder-only models at approximately 10M, 30M, and 100M parameters, Full Relation achieves lower final validation NLL than MHA at all three scales. In a fixed-context reference benchmark, FlashRelation is 3.60-4.41x faster than the materialized Full Relation implementation. Across scale-matched production workloads, it reaches 76.4-84.9% of PyTorch FlashAttention throughput while executing the Full Relation operator. Hybrid Relation uses 75% Linear Relation layers and achieves strong language-modeling quality. These results support a relation-first view of token mixing: ask Self, ask Others, then let Flow follow Relation.

</details>


### [186] [A Standardized Framework for Machine Learning in Power System Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20181)
*Julian Oelhaf,Georg Kordowich,Paula Andrea Pérez-Toro,Christian Bergler,Johann Jäger,Andreas Maier,Siming Bayer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个标准化导向的评估框架，将机器学习电力系统保护的评估设计视为科学贡献的一部分，定义了七个必需的研究维度（保护目标、物理范围、可观测性、时间与决策窗口、目标与样本有效性、验证协议、评估输出），并在PROTECT-90电磁暂态基准上进行了有界案例研究。实验显示MLP在分类上F1达0.991±0.001，定位误差10.20±0.25%线路长度；而清洁条件下的高预测性能并不能决定鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于机器学习的电力系统保护研究频繁报告接近完美的分数，但这些分数的意义强烈依赖于评估设置。保护任务、物理范围、测量、时序、目标、预处理和验证常常联合变化且未完全说明，导致结果难以比较、不可审计，缺乏标准化评估框架。

Method: 提出包含七个必需研究维度的标准化评估框架：保护目标、物理范围、可观测性、时序与决策窗口、目标与样本有效性、验证协议、评估输出。并在PROTECT-90基准（包含9022个模拟事件，90kV双回线拓扑）上实例化该框架，研究集中式感知、20ms/50ms决策窗口、episode分组验证下的故障分类与定位。

Result: 在集中式感知与20ms窗口下，MLP五折宏平均F1为0.991±0.001（分类），定位MAE为10.20±0.25%（线路长度比例）。延长至50ms决策窗保持任务不对称性；降低可观测性使MLP定位误差约翻倍但对分类影响小。同步双端传统定位器在更丰富的清洁信息下优于学习定位器；测量退化表明清洁预测性能不能决定鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架将评估假设转化为明确、可复现的证据，为更可比、可审计的评估以及未来面向认证的机器学习保护功能评估奠定基础。

Abstract: Studies of machine-learning-based power-system protection increasingly report near-perfect scores, yet the meaning of those scores depends strongly on the evaluation setting. Protection task, physical scope, measurements, timing, targets, preprocessing, and validation often vary jointly and remain incompletely specified. This paper proposes a standardization-oriented framework that treats evaluation design as part of the scientific contribution. It defines seven required study dimensions: protection objective, physical scope, observability, timing and decision windows, targets and sample validity, validation protocol, and evaluation outputs. The framework is instantiated in a bounded case study on the public PROTECT-90 electromagnetic-transient benchmark, comprising 9022 simulated episodes from a 90 kV double-line topology, for onset-conditioned fault classification and localization. Under centralized sensing, simulation-metadata-aligned 20 ms windows, and episode-grouped validation, a multi-layer perceptron (MLP) achieved a five-fold mean macro-averaged F1 score of 0.991 +/- 0.001 for classification and a localization mean absolute error of 10.20 +/- 0.25% of line length (mean +/- std across episode-grouped folds). Extending the decision horizon to 50 ms preserved this task-dependent performance asymmetry, while reduced observability approximately doubled the MLP localization error but had little effect on classification. A synchronized two-ended conventional locator outperformed the learning locators under its richer clean information set, and measurement degradation showed that clean predictive performance did not determine robustness. The framework turns evaluation assumptions into explicit, reproducible evidence and provides a basis for more comparable, auditable evaluation and future certification-oriented assessment of machine-learning protection functions.

</details>


### [187] [Exact Algebraic Computation of Learning Coefficients for Two-Dimensional Singular Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20183)
*Grégoire Sergeant-Perthuis,Elias Tsigaridas,Jules Tsukahara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个能够精确计算任意二维模型局部实对数规范阈值（RLCT）的确定性算法，该模型需满足Kullback-Leibler距离与多项式接触等价的条件。算法提供了复杂度上界，并在多项式神经网络等广泛模型中展示了有效性，可作为采样估计器的地面真值并进行校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典信息准则（如BIC）在奇异模型（如深度学习）中因正则性假设失效而导致模型选择错误。WBIC依赖的学习系数λ（解析情形下等于局部RLCT）的精确计算仅限于特殊情况，目前仅能通过采样方法近似估计。

Method: 作者提出了第一个确定性算法，用于精确计算二维模型（其KL距离与多项式接触等价）的局部RLCT。算法推导了复杂度上界，并对广泛模型类（包括多项式神经网络）进行了有效性验证。

Result: 算法在浅层模型场景中不仅提供了采样估计器的校准基准（ground truth），还揭示了采样方法无法发现的代数结构，并且在速度上超越采样方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为奇异模型的学习系数计算提供了首个精确确定性方法，弥补了精确计算与采样估计之间的空白，对深度学习等奇异模型的信息准则应用具有重要理论价值。

Abstract: Classical information criteria such as the Bayesian Information Criterion (BIC) rely on regularity assumptions that break down for singular models, leading to incorrect model selection in settings such as deep learning. The Widely Applicable Bayesian Information Criterion (WBIC) relies on local learning coefficients $λ$, which in the analytic case coincides with local Real Log Canonical Thresholds (RLCT) of the Kullback-Leibler divergence of the model, to capture correct marginal likelihood asymptotics. Exact computation of the learning coefficients has been limited to special cases, and only sampling-based estimation methods are generally applicable. We present the first deterministic algorithm that computes local RLCTs exactly for any two-dimensional model whose Kullback-Leibler distance is contact equivalent to a polynomial, derive a bound on its complexity, and demonstrate its effectiveness for a broad class of models, with applications including polynomial neural networks. Beyond providing ground truth to calibrate sampling-based estimators, exact computation reveals algebraic structure in learning coefficients that sampling cannot and out-speeds it in the shallow regime.

</details>


### [188] [Decoding silent reading from non-invasive EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20186)
*Ingo Marquardt,Anthilia Alchanat,Priyanka Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将默读作为内语音解码的可扩展代理任务，使用19通道干电极EEG记录了单个被试约49小时、24万词次的阅读数据，通过CLIP风格的对比学习训练卷积编码器（可选接因果Transformer），实现开放词表的词汇级解码。结果显示词级top-10检索显著优于随机基线，随数据量对数线性增长且未饱和，并区分了词级解码与上下文追踪及位置先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内语音的非侵入式解码面临根本性数据问题：无法收集与自发音内心独白配对的脑活动数据，现有代理范式（线索重复和回溯报告生成式内语音）采集慢、时间对齐差且无法验证被试依从性。因此需要一种可扩展的代理任务来研究词汇信息能否从神经信号中解码。

Method: 采用默读作为可扩展代理任务。实验使用19通道干电极EEG，单被试密集采样约49小时393次运行，共约24万词次。词以快速序列视觉呈现（RSVP）方式展示，每个试验随机化字体排印以去相关词身份与低层视觉形态。训练一个卷积EEG编码器（可选接因果Transformer），使用CLIP风格对比目标，将短EEG窗口与大型语言模型提供的目标词隐状态嵌入对齐。评估采用词分组top-10检索对比排列基线，并设计控制实验分离词级解码与上下文追踪及位置先验。

Result: 解码结果可靠高于机会水平，扩展到中频和稀有词，随训练数据量对数线性增长且无饱和迹象。移除枕叶和后颞电极使词级增益减少约三分之一，但上下文追踪未受影响。控制分析成功将词级解码与叙事上下文追踪及Transformer位置嵌入引入的非神经位置先验分离。

Conclusion: 研究证实了开放词表级别的词汇信息可以从默读期间的EEG中恢复，且当前解码受限于数据量而非算法饱和。这为未来以内语音为目标的可扩展神经解码研究提供了方法论基础和数据支持。

Abstract: Non-invasive decoding of inner speech faces a fundamental data problem: a corpus pairing brain activity with a person's spontaneous inner monologue cannot be collected, and the available proxy paradigms (cued repetitive and retrospectively reported generative inner speech) are slow to acquire, poorly time-locked, and subject compliance is unverifiable. We therefore treat silent reading as a scalable proxy task and ask how much lexical and semantic information a contrastive decoder can extract from it. We report an open-vocabulary analysis of approximately 240,000 word presentations recorded from a single densely-sampled participant across 393 runs (ca. 49 h) of 19-channel dry-electrode EEG. Words from continuous narrative text were presented in rapid serial visual presentation, with typography randomised on every trial to partially decorrelate word identity from low-level visual form. A convolutional EEG encoder, optionally followed by a causal transformer, was trained with a CLIP-style contrastive objective to align short EEG windows with hidden-state embeddings of the presented word taken from a large language model. Decoding, evaluated as word-grouped top-10 retrieval against permutation baselines, was reliably above chance, extended to mid-frequency and rare words, and scaled log-linearly with training-data volume with no sign of saturation. Removing occipital and posterior-temporal electrodes reduced the word-level gain by roughly one third but left context tracking unchanged. Control analyses separate word-level decoding from narrative context tracking and from a non-neural positional prior introduced by the transformer's positional embedding. These results establish that open-vocabulary word-level information is recoverable from EEG during silent reading, and that decoding is data-limited rather than saturated.

</details>


### [189] [DICS: Data-Informed Centroid Splitting for Decision Tree Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20258)
*MD Saifur Rahman Mazumder,Feng Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出数据信息质心分裂（DICS）框架，利用聚类先验构造紧凑的候选分裂集，在保持分类树性能的同时显著降低训练时间，并提供了理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策树模型虽然可解释性强且性能优异，但在大型高维数据集上训练成本高昂，主要原因是每个节点都需要对所有候选分裂进行穷举搜索。作者希望利用数据驱动的先验信息来减少搜索空间，从而提升训练效率。

Method: 提出DICS（Data-Informed Centroid Splitting）框架，通过聚类方法构造基于数据先验的紧凑候选分裂集，将类别感知结构融入分裂选择过程，可集成到分类树、随机森林和梯度提升模型中。同时提供了理论分析证明该框架在给定假设下不会降低分类树的性能。

Result: 在合成数据集和基准数据集上的大量实验表明，DICS在保持与穷举搜索相当的分类精度的同时，大幅减少了训练时间。

Conclusion: 将数据信息先验融入分裂选择过程，可以在不牺牲分类性能的前提下实现可扩展的分类树学习，为大规模决策树训练提供了一种高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Decision tree-based models are widely used in machine learning due to their interpretability and strong empirical performance. However, training decision trees can be computationally expensive, particularly for large and high-dimensional datasets, largely due to the exhaustive search over candidate splits at each node. To improve computational efficiency, we propose Data-Informed Centroid Splitting (DICS), a clustering-based framework that constructs a compact and informative set of candidate splits using data-driven priors. By incorporating class-aware structure, DICS significantly reduces the split search space for classification tasks while preserving predictive performance. We further provide theoretical analysis showing that under the stated assumptions, DICS does not degrade the performance of classification trees compared to exhaustive split search. DICS can be incorporated into classification trees, random forests, and gradient-boosting models. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DICS achieves comparable accuracy while substantially reducing training time across synthetic and benchmark datasets, highlighting the benefit of integrating data-informed priors into split selection for scalable classification tree learning.

</details>


### [190] [Dynamic Structural Causal Modeling for Sleep](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20285)
*Ranveer Singh,Saurabh Mathur,Pranuthi Tenali,Arun Badi,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用PCMCI+算法从家庭睡眠呼吸暂停测试（HSAT）记录中学习睡眠呼吸障碍的动态因果图，揭示了不同性别和年龄亚群之间因果结构的系统性差异。通过边缘黑名单利用领域知识，并使用bootstrap聚合处理小样本问题，发现时间自依赖和呼吸暂停-去饱和关系在所有群体中持续存在，而其他关系则变化显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 睡眠呼吸障碍的因果动力学复杂且在不同患者群体间存在差异，这阻碍了针对性干预措施的开发。需要理解其因果结构以支持精准医疗。

Method: 使用PCMCI+算法对来自105个HSAT记录的窗口化分数变量进行动态因果图学习；通过边缘黑名单利用领域知识；采用bootstrap聚合以应对较小的亚群样本量。

Result: 学习得到的因果图显示，时间自依赖关系和呼吸暂停-去饱和关系在所有群体中保持一致，而其他变量之间的关系则在不同性别和年龄亚群间存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 不同患者群体的睡眠呼吸障碍因果结构存在系统性差异，但存在跨群体共有的核心因果模式，这为开发群体特异性干预措施提供了依据。

Abstract: The causal dynamics of sleep-disordered breathing are complex and vary across patient populations, hindering the development of targeted interventions. We learn dynamic causal graphs of sleep-disordered breathing from Home Sleep Apnea Test (HSAT) recordings, revealing systematic differences in causal structure across sex and age subcohorts. We do so using the PCMCI+ algorithm on windowed fractional variables derived from 105 HSAT recordings, exploiting domain knowledge via edge blacklisting and employing bootstrap aggregation to address small subcohort sizes. The learned graphs show that temporal self-dependencies and the apnea-desaturation relationship persist across all cohorts, while other relationships vary substantially.

</details>


### [191] [Physical-Support Confidence Sets for Highly Coherent Dictionaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20295)
*Guan-Ju Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分辨率感知的物理支撑推断方法，用于解决字典学习后稀疏追踪在高度相干字典中物理解释不明确的问题。通过交叉字典置信对应方法，同时考虑学习字典的不确定性和部署信号表示的不确定性，并在物理支撑空间上投影存活的解释。提出了主动端点括入（AEB）算法，一种自适应有限组过程，只评估可能影响物理报告的候选，否则安全粗化或弃权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 字典学习后的稀疏追踪即使其物理解释未被校准数据证明，也可能产生精确的原子支撑，尤其是在高度相干的字典中，不同的校准兼容字典可能对相同的选定支撑赋予不同的物理含义。因此需要对字典学习和部署信号表示中的不确定性进行联合处理。

Method: 提出交叉字典置信对应方法，保留校准兼容字典和部署兼容稀疏表示，然后将存活的解释投影到物理支撑空间。对于局部相干原子类（分离尺度s），推导了最小极大物理分辨率。引入主动端点括入（AEB）算法，一种自适应有限组过程，仅评估仍可能影响物理报告的候选。

Result: 从N个校准信号获得的最小极大物理分辨率满足 δ_opt(N,s)≍min{s,1/(√N·s²)}，相对分辨率由方向信息尺度Ns⁶控制。部署重复仅在方向变化不能通过调整活跃系数吸收时改善物理定位。有限组实验（包括四区域合成应用）表明点值插件选择器在物理上可能过度精确，而AEB避免了不支持的细化且需要更少的候选评估。

Conclusion: AEB算法能够避免点值估计方法中的物理过度精确问题，通过自适应评估候选和安全粗化/弃权机制，以更少的候选评估实现更可靠的分辨率感知物理支撑推断。

Abstract: Sparse pursuit after dictionary learning can yield a precise atom support even when its physical interpretation is not justified by the calibration data, especially for highly coherent dictionaries where alternative calibration-compatible dictionaries may assign different physical meanings to the same selected support. We develop resolution-aware physical-support inference that jointly accounts for uncertainty in the learned dictionary and in the representation of a deployment signal. Our cross-dictionary confidence correspondence retains calibration-compatible dictionaries and deployment-compatible sparse representations, then projects the surviving explanations onto physical-support space. For local coherent-atom classes with separation scale s, once the deployment data resolve the coherent-block explanation and its atom support, the minimax physical resolution from N calibration signals satisfies $δ_{\mathrm{opt}}(N,s)\asymp\min\{s,\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}s^2}\}$, with relative resolution governed by the orientation-information scale $Ns^6$. Deployment replication improves physical localization only when orientation changes cannot be absorbed by adjusting the active coefficients. For computation, we introduce active endpoint bracketing (AEB), an adaptive finite-bank procedure that evaluates only candidates that can still affect the physical report and otherwise safely coarsens or abstains. Finite-bank experiments, including a four-region synthetic application, show that a point-valued plug-in selector can be physically overprecise, whereas AEB avoids unsupported refinement with fewer candidate evaluations.

</details>


### [192] [Explainable Transformer Models for Clinical Prediction Tasks on Structured Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20315)
*Jun Ni Du,Lukas Adamek,Maxim Kryukov,Flavio Dormont,Ziv Bar-Joseph,Sven Jager,Brandon Rufino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BERT-LER，一种基于BERT架构的编码EHR时间线模型，通过百分位分箱将实验室检验结果编码为离散token，并结合Integrated Gradients实现token级别的可解释性。在7500万患者的去标识化EHR数据上预训练和微调，在EHRShot基准和哮喘严重程度进展研究中验证，性能与现有基准模型相当且在实验室相关任务上更优，解释与临床已知风险因素一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对结构化EHR的预测模型很少有同时强调定量实验室信息和对输入医学事件的可解释性的方法，存在方法论空白。

Method: 采用BERT风格架构处理编码EHR时间线；通过基于百分位的分箱将实验室检验结果编码为离散token以保留分级信息；使用Integrated Gradients进行token级归因解释；在7500万患者数据上预训练并微调。

Result: 在EHRShot基准和哮喘进展任务上，BERT-LER的预测性能与公开基准模型相当，且在涉及实验室相关任务时往往超越基准；其归因结果与临床已知风险因素一致。

Conclusion: BERT-LER架构和解释方法可推广到多种治疗领域和预测任务，适用于基于结构化EHR训练的语言模型，弥合了实验室值表示与可解释性统一框架的缺口。

Abstract: Predictive models over structured electronic health records (EHRs) remain central to machine learning for healthcare, but few have jointly emphasized quantitative laboratory information and interpretability with respect to input medical events. We present BERT-LER, a BERT-style model for coded EHR timelines pretrained and fine-tuned from a de-identified EHR dataset of 75 million patients, that encodes laboratory test results as discrete tokens while retaining graded information through percentile-based binning, paired with Integrated Gradients for token-level attributions grounded in the input EHR sequence. We benchmark our approach on the public EHRShot benchmark suite and on an asthma severity progression study based on real-world data. This addresses a methodological gap in EHR foundation-style modeling by unifying laboratory value representation and explainability in a single framework, while assessing whether both predictive performance and explanations generalize beyond standard clinical prediction tasks. Across EHRShot and asthma tasks, BERT-LER achieves predictive performance that is competitive with, and on laboratory-related tasks often exceeds, publicly available benchmark models, and provides attributions that align with clinically known risk factors. Our architecture and explainability approach can be applied to many therapeutic areas and prediction tasks using language models trained on structured EHRs.

</details>


### [193] [A comparison between ceiling-mounted FMCW, IR-UWB and Wi-Fi radar for in-bedroom human activity monitoring and sleep interruption detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20322)
*Anton Lambrecht,Reda El Hail,Xianjun Jiao,Pieter Crombez,Dominique Schreurs,Peter Karsmakers,Adnan Shahid,Eli De Poorter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在相同部署条件下（20名参与者、6种房间布局、同一CNN架构）对天花板安装的FMCW雷达、IR-UWB雷达和Wi-Fi感知三种射频技术进行了受控对比。IR-UWB在跨受试者活动识别中表现最佳（89.0%宏F1），FMCW对未知房间布局泛化最好（83.8%宏F1），睡眠监测任务中所有技术均超过92%宏F1，并揭示了识别性能与环境鲁棒性之间的根本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同射频技术（FMCW、IR-UWB、Wi-Fi）在医疗健康监测中虽日益重要，但现有研究往往因硬件、数据集和评估方法不同而难以在相同条件下公平对比；此外天花板安装雷达的实际部署优势尚未被充分探索。

Method: 采用受控实验设计，使用20名参与者在6种房间布局下进行同步记录，以相同CNN架构对三种技术（FMCW、IR-UWB、Wi-Fi）进行评估，涵盖细粒度10类人类活动识别任务和粗粒度4类睡眠监测任务，并从距离分辨率、天线分集、多普勒分辨率和空间信息保留等维度分析性能差异。

Result: IR-UWB在跨受试者活动识别中达到89.0%宏F1，FMCW在跨房间布局泛化中达到83.8%宏F1；睡眠监测方面所有技术在未知环境中均超过92%宏F1。发现识别性能与环境鲁棒性之间存在根本性权衡。

Conclusion: 三种射频技术在识别性能与环境鲁棒性间存在固有平衡，该权衡可通过物理层特性（距离/多普勒分辨率、天线分集、空间信息）解释，为面向医疗的射频感知系统设计提供了实用指导准则。

Abstract: Despite their growing importance for contact-free radio frequency (RF) based healthcare monitoring, different radio technologies such as frequency-modulated continuous wave (FMCW) radar, impulse radio ultra-wideband (IR-UWB), and Wi-Fi sensing are rarely compared under identical deployment conditions, as existing studies typically differ in hardware, datasets, and evaluation methodologies. In addition, the performance of ceiling-mounted radars, despite their practical deployment and cost advantages in healthcare environments, remain underexplored. Therefore, this paper presents a controlled comparison and analysis of ceiling-mounted FMCW, IR-UWB, and Wi-Fi sensing using synchronized recordings from 20 participants across six room layouts. All technologies are evaluated with the same convolutional neural network (CNN) on both a fine-grained 10-class human activity recognition (HAR) task and a coarse 4-class sleep monitoring task. IR-UWB achieves the highest cross-subject activity recognition performance (89.0% macro F1), while FMCW generalizes best to unseen room layouts (83.8% macro F1). For sleep monitoring, all technologies exceed 92% macro F1 in unseen environments. The results reveal a fundamental trade-off between recognition performance and environmental robustness, which can be explained through differences in range resolution, antenna diversity, Doppler resolution, and spatial information retention. These findings provide practical guidelines for the design of healthcare-oriented RF sensing systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [194] [Active Spiking Perception: The Membrane Potential as a Belief State for Anytime 3D Point Cloud Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19232)
*Akarsh Jain,Arya Pawa,Ayush Debnath,Smera Rawal,Sayeed Shafayet Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: ASP将3D点云识别重构为迭代决策过程，利用LIF膜电位作为类别信念，通过轻量级切片选择策略动态选择观测块并支持置信度早退，在ModelNet40/10上达到90.62%和93.28%，并能迁移至稠密预测任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脉冲点云网络以固定、与输入无关的顺序扫描空间，未利用脉冲计算最具特色的膜电位时间演化作为决策依据，因此缺乏自适应的感知决策能力。

Method: 将3D识别重构为迭代决策过程：LIF膜电位作为类别运行信念选择下一观测块，Slice-Selection Policy基于膜状态和几何描述符打分未访问的最远点采样块，通过直通Gumbel-Softmax端到端训练，推理时退化为argmax；支持置信度早退和流式状态前向传递。

Result: ModelNet40和ModelNet10上分别达90.62%和93.28%，比最强脉冲基线低1.7点但骨干更小；ShapeNetPart达83.21实例mIoU，S3DIS Area 5达48.50 mIoU（首次脉冲结果）；能耗降低1.35x至2.8x。

Conclusion: ASP证明了膜电位时间演化可作为有效的决策载体，策略不限于脉冲骨干（可迁移到注视式非脉冲Transformer），同时从理论层面证明了漏电积分是贝叶斯滤波的递归对数后验更新，早退规则满足无分布选择风险。

Abstract: Spiking point cloud networks usually scan space in a fixed, input-agnostic order, which leaves the most distinctive resource of spiking computation, the temporal evolution of the membrane potential, unused as a locus of decision-making. Active Spiking Perception (ASP) recasts 3D recognition as an iterative decision process in which the network's own leaky integrate-and-fire (LIF) membrane potential, read as a running belief over the class, selects the next chunk to observe and triggers confidence-margin early exit. A lightweight Slice-Selection Policy scores unvisited farthest-point-sampled chunks from the membrane state and precomputed geometric descriptors, trains end-to-end through a straight-through Gumbel-Softmax, reduces to an argmax at inference, and adds about 2% of backbone parameters. We prove that leaky integration is the recursive log-posterior update of a Bayesian filter, that the exit rule attains distribution-free selective risk with no multiple-testing penalty at the stopping time, and that streaming state carry-forward is exactly equivalent to prefix recomputation with bounded finite-precision drift. ASP reaches 90.62% and 93.28% on ModelNet40 and ModelNet10, 1.7 points below the strongest spiking baseline at a larger backbone, while adding a certified anytime interface no baseline offers. The mechanism transfers unchanged to dense prediction, giving 83.21 instance mIoU on ShapeNetPart and 48.50 mIoU on S3DIS Area 5, to our knowledge the first spiking results on S3DIS Area 5, and, fixation replacing chunk selection, to a foveated non-spiking transformer, so the policy is not tied to spiking backbones: cost is exactly linear in observations and the threshold is a measured compute dial spanning 2.8x to 1.35x less energy. One limitation is concrete: one S3DIS class is unidentifiable at the crop size we use, and we give the prediction that would fix it.

</details>


### [195] [Spiking Local Interaction and Adaptive Complementary Fusion for Spiking Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19238)
*Dongcheng Zhao,Sicheng Shen,Zhenyu Yang,Zhiyuan Li,Jinyan Yu,Yongjian Wang,Tiechui Yao,Wenli Zhang,Tielin Zhang*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出Spiking Local Interaction (SLI)和Adaptive Complementary Fusion (ACF)两种模块来解决Spiking Transformer中二进制注意力机制抑制弱关系和限制局部空间上下文传播的问题。SLI提供注意力独立的相邻token局部信息交换路径，ACF通过层特定通道级系数自适应融合SSA和SLI。在多个数据集上取得一致性改进，其中QKFormer在ImageNet-1K上达84.37% Top-1准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Spiking Transformer通过脉冲自注意力(SSA)建模token交互，但二进制查询和键表示将连续相似度映射为稀疏离散关系响应，这会抑制弱关系并限制局部空间上下文的传播，影响模型的表达能力和性能。

Method: 1) SLI (Spiking Local Interaction)：使用轻量级深度卷积-逐点卷积变换，建立注意力无关的相邻脉冲token直接信息交换路径。2) ACF (Adaptive Complementary Fusion)：通过层特定、通道级系数在不同网络深度自适应平衡SSA和SLI的贡献。该方法保留原始注意力公式，可作为插件集成到不同Spiking Transformer架构中，参数开销小。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K、CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、CIFAR10-DVS和ADE20K上，图像分类、事件驱动识别和语义分割任务均获得一致性提升。QKFormer+SLI+ACF在ImageNet-1K上达84.37% Top-1准确率，ADE20K上达37.5% mIoU（无需ImageNet预训练）。消融实验表明SSA和SLI捕获互补交互模式，可学习融合优于固定加权。

Conclusion: 提出SLI和ACF有效缓解了Spiking Transformer中二进制注意力对弱关系抑制和局部上下文传播受限的问题。该方法模块化设计、参数开销小、架构通用，在多种任务上验证了有效性。SSA和SLI的互补性以及自适应融合的优势为未来脉冲Transformer设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Spiking Transformers model token interactions primarily through spiking self-attention (SSA). However, binary query and key representations map continuous similarities to sparse and discrete relation responses, which may suppress weak relations and limit the propagation of local spatial context. To address this limitation, we introduce Spiking Local Interaction (SLI) and Adaptive Complementary Fusion (ACF). SLI establishes an attention-independent pathway for direct information exchange among neighboring spiking tokens using lightweight depthwise--pointwise transformations. ACF integrates SSA and SLI through layer-specific, channel-wise coefficients that adaptively balance their contributions at different network depths. The proposed design preserves the original attention formulation and can be incorporated into different Spiking Transformer architectures with modest parameter overhead. Experiments on ImageNet-1K, CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, CIFAR10-DVS, and ADE20K show consistent improvements across image classification, event-based recognition, and semantic segmentation. In particular, QKFormer with SLI and ACF achieves $84.37\%$ Top-1 accuracy on ImageNet-1K and $37.5\%$ mIoU on ADE20K, where the segmentation model is trained without ImageNet pretraining. Ablation studies and qualitative analyses further indicate that SSA and SLI capture complementary interaction patterns and that learnable fusion consistently outperforms fixed weighting.

</details>


### [196] [BASC : Behavior-Aligned Quantization and Pruning for Low-Bit Spiking Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19239)
*Linliang Chen,Yan Zhong,Xin Liu,Sai Li,Wang Kang*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出BASC框架，通过时间行为尺度修正（TSC）和边界级通道间修正（BIC）两个轻量模块，统一解决SNN压缩中量化与剪枝的标准-行为不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能SNN在资源受限设备上部署面临存储和时间步计算成本高的挑战。现有量化与剪枝方法采用局部准则做出决策，量化忽略时序任务反馈，剪枝忽略通道间依赖，导致压缩效果次优。

Method: 提出行为对齐SNN压缩（BASC）框架，包含两个模块：（1）TSC：将量化尺度应用于每个时间步的突触电流从而影响脉冲时序，在时间损失下使尺度可学习，让放电行为参与尺度优化；（2）BIC：先用通道级重要性分数进行初始选择，再对接近剪枝阈值的通道利用通道间信息重新评估。

Result: 在静态和神经形态基准上的大量实验表明，低位宽BASC模型能够匹配或超越高位宽基线，且在结构化剪枝后仍保持精度优势，同时进一步减少模型存储和突触计算量。

Conclusion: BASC通过将时序行为反馈融入量化、将通道间依赖引入剪枝，有效解决了标准-行为不匹配问题，实现了更优的SNN压缩性能。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) encode information through binary spikes and compute in an event-driven manner, offering an energy-efficient paradigm for machine intelligence. However, high-performance SNNs incur substantial memory and timestep-wise computation costs that hinder deployment on resource-constrained devices. Quantization and pruning provide complementary routes to reducing these costs, yet both make their decisions with local criteria that overlook temporal task feedback in quantization and inter-channel dependencies in pruning. Consequently, optimizing either criterion can still yield suboptimal compression performance. We refer to this discrepancy as criterion-behavior mismatch and propose Behavior-Aligned SNN Compression (BASC), a unified framework with two lightweight modules. For quantization, the scale is applied to synaptic current at every timestep and therefore shifts spike timing. Temporal-Behavior Scale Correction (TSC) makes the scale learnable under a temporal loss, allowing firing behavior to inform scale optimization. For pruning, channel importance depends on how channels jointly drive the membrane potential across the firing threshold. Boundary-Level Inter-Channel Correction (BIC) uses channelwise importance scores for initial selection and inter-channel information to re-evaluate only channels near the pruning threshold. Extensive experiments on static and neuromorphic benchmarks show that lower-bit BASC models match or outperform higher-bit baselines and retain this accuracy advantage after structured pruning, while further reducing model storage and synaptic operations.

</details>


### [197] [The Forward-Backward Disconnect: State Dynamics, Credit Assignment, and Biological Grounding in Neural Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19995)
*Hadi Al Mubasher,Mariette Awad*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文指出现代神经网络的前向计算已大幅多样化（循环、注意力、状态空间、连续时间、隐式、脉冲等），但训练机制仍集中于反向传播及其变体，形成"前向-反向断连"问题，并提出一个涵盖状态动力学结构、信用分配机制和生物基础三个耦合轴的系统分类框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经计算的前向结构不断演化并重新引入动力学和生物结构，但可扩展的训练方法仍几乎全部依赖反向传播类梯度误差传播机制。作者认为这一不对称性（前向-反向断连）是阻碍神经网络发展的关键问题，需要通过系统化分析来理解其严重性与弥合路径。

Method: 提出一个分类体系（taxonomy），沿三个耦合轴对神经模型家族进行分类：状态动力学结构、信用分配机制、生物基础。将前向计算的基础性与学习的基础性分开考量，以"架构-学习配置"（architecture-learning configuration）而非单一架构名称作为分析单元。覆盖静态、循环、注意力、状态空间、连续时间、隐式、脉冲、生物合理及神经形态等模型家族。

Result: 分析显示：前向动力学在上述各类模型家族中已高度多样化，但达到最高可扩展规模的学习机制仍然集中在全局或紧密源自梯度的误差传播机制（如BP、BPTT、伴随法、隐式微分、替代梯度）。这一断连在各类模型家族中普遍存在。

Conclusion: 弥合前向-反向断连需要更好地对齐状态动力学、信用分配机制与计算载体（substrate）三者之间的关系，从而推动训练方法的多样化与生物学合理性。

Abstract: A recurring pattern in neural computation is the reintroduction of dynamical and biological structure into models originally simplified for scalable optimization. Early feedforward networks reduced biological neurons to threshold or rate-like summation units, an abstraction compatible with global-gradient training at scale. Since then, forward computation has diversified: modern architectures carry recurrent state, retrieve from long contexts through attention and associative memory, compress histories through structured state-space dynamics, evolve in continuous time, settle to implicit equilibria, and communicate through sparse spikes.
  Training has diversified less. Scalable learning remains concentrated around backpropagation, backpropagation through time, adjoint methods, implicit differentiation, and surrogate-gradient variants. We call this asymmetry the forward-backward disconnect and develop a taxonomy spanning neural model families along three coupled axes: state-dynamics structure, credit-assignment mechanism, and biological grounding. Forward and learning grounding are treated separately, and the unit of analysis is the architecture-learning configuration rather than the architecture name alone.
  Across static, recurrent, attention-based, state-space, continuous-time, implicit, spiking, biologically plausible, and neuromorphic families, forward dynamics have diversified while the highest demonstrated scales remain concentrated in global or closely gradient-derived error-propagation mechanisms. Closing this disconnect requires better alignment among state dynamics, credit assignment, and computational substrate.

</details>


### [198] [Petri Net Description of Biological Neural Circuits for Fast Hardware Prototyping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20147)
*Carlo daCunha,Rodrigo Pena,Marcos Turqueti*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于T-时序Petri网的生物神经回路建模方法，通过形式化可验证的时序语义实现截止时间保证的实时执行，并独立于积分时间步长地与连续时间漏积分-发放动力学建立解析对应关系。在三个模拟微回路（反馈抑制、侧抑制、层级特征检测器）中验证了模型正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物神经回路仿真方法（无论是通用硬件还是专用神经形态平台）受限于固定时间步长数值积分、硬件精度限制，且无法在实时约束下保证事件驱动脉冲动力学的时序正确性。这严重制约了神经回路仿真的实时性和可靠性。

Method: 将神经元、突触和脉冲事件建模为T-时序Petri网，该模型具有形式化可验证的时序语义，能够提供截止时间保证的实时执行，并与连续时间漏积分-发放（LIF）动力学建立解析对应关系，其对应性不依赖于底层积分时间步长。

Result: 在三个模拟微回路（反馈抑制、侧抑制、层级特征检测器）中，Petri神经元均再现了各回路预期的动力学特征，同时在整个过程中提供形式化有界的时序保证，三种情况下的最坏情况响应时间均与分析预测一致。

Conclusion: Petri网描述为生物神经回路仿真提供了一种克服传统数值积分限制的新范式，兼具形式化时序验证与实时执行保证能力，为神经形态计算和实时神经仿真提供了更可靠的理论基础。

Abstract: Current approaches to simulating biological neural circuits, whether on general-purpose hardware or dedicated neuromorphic platforms, remain constrained by fixed-timestep numerical integration, hardware-imposed precision limits, and an inability to guarantee timing correctness for event-driven spiking dynamics under real-time constraints. Here, we propose a Petri net description of biological neural circuits that overcomes these limitations by modeling neurons, synapses, and spike events as a T-timed Petri net with formally verifiable timing semantics, enabling deadline-guaranteed real-time execution and analytically tractable correspondence to continuous-time leak-integrate-and-fire dynamics, independent of the underlying integration timestep. To test the model, we present the results of three simulated microcircuits: feedback inhibition, lateral inhibition, and hierarchical feature detector. The Petri neuron reproduces the expected dynamical signatures of each circuit while providing formally bounded timing guarantees throughout, with worst-case response times matching analytical predictions across all three cases.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [199] [The Evaluation Context Protocol (ECP): A Portable Contract for AI Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19263)
*Aniket Wattamwar,Manav Anandani,Mrunal Kakirwar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出评估上下文协议（ECP），一个早期阶段、厂商中立的框架，旨在作为智能体系统的可移植评估契约层，通过定义JSON-RPC接口来统一评估跨框架的AI代理，并提供了包括LangChain、LlamaIndex等适配器的开源参考实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI从孤立LLM评估转向自主智能体架构评估，现有评估范式存在严重局限：基准被利用、"自信但错误"现象、理论能力与实际运维可靠性之间的差距。评估基础设施碎片化问题亟需解决。

Method: 提出ECP（Evaluation Context Protocol）框架，定义了一个小型JSON-RPC接口，使智能体能够暴露其用户可见输出、工具调用和评估者安全的审计上下文，并允许在框架和CI系统中统一运行程序化检查。提供了针对LangChain、LlamaIndex、CrewAI和PydanticAI的开源参考实现。

Result: 论文呈现了ECP作为工作进展而非最终标准的形态，描述了包含多框架适配器的开源实现，并针对近期文献中记录的失效模式进行了设计定位。经验验证仍列为未来工作。

Conclusion: ECP被定位为初期协议而非定稿标准，评估面、方法集和评分器族预计会随协议在更多系统中实践而演变，实证验证是未来工作的重点。

Abstract: The evolution of artificial intelligence has necessitated a fundamental shift from evaluating isolated Large Language Models (LLMs) to assessing autonomous agentic architectures. This paper explores the critical methodologies for evaluating AI agents and the essential role of advanced observability infrastructure. We analyze the architectural components of agents and identify the severe limitations of current evaluation paradigms, including benchmark exploitation, the "confidently wrong" phenomenon, and the discrepancy between theoretical capability and operational reliability. To begin addressing the fragmentation in current evaluation infrastructure, this paper proposes the Evaluation Context Protocol (ECP), an early-stage, vendor-neutral framework intended to act as a portable evaluation contract layer for agentic systems. In its current form ECP defines a small JSON-RPC interface over which an agent exposes its user-visible output, the tool calls it made, and evaluator-safe audit context, and against which programmatic checks can be run uniformly across frameworks and continuous integration systems. We describe an open-source reference implementation that includes adapters for LangChain, LlamaIndex, CrewAI, and PydanticAI, and we situate the design against failure modes documented in the recent literature. ECP is presented as work in progress rather than a finished standard: the evaluation surface, method set, and grader families are all expected to change as the protocol is exercised against more systems, and the empirical validation required to justify adoption is outlined as future work.

</details>


### [200] [Stable Within, Unidentified Across: Semantic Identification of Benchmark Effects and Rankings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19269)
*Xi Qin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文指出评估结论依赖于评估者控制的语义（法律引用、可评分性和聚合方式）。通过一个217行的冻结分析，作者展示了评估语义的不变性（evaluation-semantic identification）在受限制的合同族内成立，但在更广泛的族中不成立。在TraceElephant案例中，F_asym族产生精确的-40.76到-43.03百分点的任务分离采用效应，而F_cc族中两个冻结的对比可比处理精确产生零效应（[0,0]区间）。二者并集F_audit无法识别T，尽管F_cc单独能识别T=0。因子审计将100%的分歧归因于单侧终端删除，非终端贡献为零。排名在不同对称处理间仍依赖规格。这是一个局部化的非蕴涵见证和可复用的族索引审计，而非流行率估计、穷尽语义分类法或独立复制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示评估结论对评估者控制的语义选择（如法律引用、可评分性、聚合规则）的敏感性，指出即使数据冻结，语义族的选择也会导致截然不同甚至完全相反的结论。

Method: 采用族索引审计（family-indexed audit）方法，对比不同语义族（F_asym、F_cc、F_audit）下的评估结果，并通过因子审计（factorial audit）分解各语义因素对分歧的贡献。

Result: F_asym显示-40.76至-43.03个百分点的显著负效应；F_cc显示精确为零（[0,0]区间）；二者并集F_audit无法识别T。因子分析显示100%分歧来自单侧终端删除，非终端贡献为零。

Conclusion: 评估结论的语义依赖性是一个局部化的非蕴涵现象：F_cc识别T=0但并不蕴涵F_audit也识别T。作者明确限定该审计不是流行率估计、穷尽分类法或独立复制，而是可复用的方法论工具。

Abstract: Evaluation conclusions depend on evaluator-controlled semantics: legal references, scoreability, and aggregation. We call an artifact-defined endpoint Q(s) evaluation-semantically identified when it is invariant over a declared family. A frozen 217-row analysis appears stable within its restricted contract family. In TraceElephant, F_asym yields precise task-disjoint adoption effects from -40.76 to -43.03 percentage points, whereas both jointly frozen contrast-comparable treatments in F_cc yield exactly zero with [0, 0] intervals. Their union F_audit therefore does not identify T, even though F_cc identifies T = 0. A factorial audit attributes 100 percent of the disagreement to one-sided terminal deletion, with zero nonterminal contribution. Rankings remain specification-dependent across the symmetric treatments. This is a localized non-implication witness and reusable family-indexed audit, not a prevalence estimate, exhaustive semantic taxonomy, or independent replication.

</details>


### [201] [Hype Meets Reality: Large Language Models as Mutators in Search-based Automated Program Repair of Simulink-Stateflow Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19347)
*Ayesha Irshad,Pablo Valle,Jon Ayerdi,Aitor Arrieta*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了将大语言模型(LLM)生成的突变算子集成到基于搜索的自动程序修复(APR)中，特别是在Simulink/Stateflow建模的网宇物理系统(CPS)场景下。通过扩展FlowRepair方法，用LLM突变替换部分原有算子，在19个真实故障模型上的受控实验表明：与预期相反，LLM突变的集成显著降低了修复性能（plausible patch从18降至4-6，valid patch从16降至4）。分析揭示LLM在精确符号编辑、行为反馈和搜索空间探索方面存在根本性局限，但作者强调这并非LLM在APR中的普遍局限，而是朴素集成的失败，并呼吁采用结构化突变与生成式引导相结合的混合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于搜索的APR技术依赖精心设计的突变算子来探索候选修复空间，而LLM的进步暗示其可动态生成修复建议以替代传统算子。作者希望验证这一假设在CPS系统的Simulink/Stateflow模型中的有效性。

Method: 将状态最先进的FlowRepair方法中的部分突变算子替换为LLM生成的突变，在相同的墙钟时间预算下，使用19个横跨四个CPS领域的真实故障Stateflow模型进行受控对比实验，测试多个LLM变体。

Result: LLM突变方法仅对4-6个模型产生plausible patches、4个模型产生valid patches，而原始FlowRepair分别达到18和16。相比原始方法，LLM集成导致修复性能显著下降。

Conclusion: 负结果揭示了朴素地将LLM集成到搜索式APR中的根本局限：LLM难以进行精确符号编辑、缺乏行为反馈机制、产生嘈杂的搜索空间阻碍探索。但这不是LLM用于APR的一般性失败，而是提示需要结合结构化突变与生成式引导的混合策略。

Abstract: Search-based Automated Program Repair (APR) techniques rely on carefully designed mutation operators to explore the space of candidate fixes. Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) suggest that generative models could replace such operators by dynamically proposing repairs. In this paper, we investigate this hypothesis in the context of Cyber-Physical Systems (CPSs) modeled in Simulink/Stateflow. We extend the state-of-the-art FlowRepair approach by replacing a subset of its mutation operators with LLM-generated mutations, enabling more flexible and expressive patch generation. We evaluate the approach on a benchmark of 19 real-world faulty Stateflow models across four CPS domains, using the same experimental setup as FlowRepair for controlled comparison under the same wall-clock budget. Contrary to expectations, in this controlled evaluation, the LLM-based mutation substantially degrades repair performance under the FlowRepair experimental setup. Across the tested LLM variants, the LLM-based repair produced plausible patches for 4-6 models and valid patches for 4 models, compared to 18 and 16, respectively, with the original approach. Our analysis reveals that, in this integration, LLMs struggle with precise symbolic edits, lack behavioral feedback, and generate a noisy search space that hinders effective exploration. Rather than showing a general limitation of LLMs for APR, these findings highlight fundamental limitations of naively integrating LLMs into search-based APR and motivate hybrid approaches that combine structured mutation with generative guidance.

</details>


### [202] [Accelerated Genetic Programming Hyper-Heuristics for Simulation-Based Scheduling via Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19487)
*Heyang Thomas Li,Alexander Pletzer,Yuan Tian,Yi Mei,Mengjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种使用Claude代理AI对Python离散事件项目调度仿真代码进行系统性重构的方法。在HPC环境中，该方法通过基准测试和正确性检查指导代理识别瓶颈、实施优化并评估效果，将测试运行时间从1,298秒降至200秒以下，且不改变输出结果，每年节省400万核时（约NZ$320,000）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python虽广泛用于科研，但定制化研究代码在大规模实验中可能极慢，尤其是在离散事件项目调度仿真中，顺序状态更新、嵌套循环、条件评估和面向对象结构限制了编译数值库和GPU加速库的优势。传统优化需要反复剖析、重构、测试和验证，而研究人员往往缺乏时间或专业的软件工程技能进行底层优化。

Method: 采用Claude代理AI对真实世界项目调度工作负载进行系统性重构。该方法以代表性基准测试和正确性检查为指导，代理AI自动识别性能瓶颈、实施针对性优化并评估优化效果，同时保留研究人员的最终控制权。

Result: 测试运行时间从1,298秒减少到200秒以下，输出结果保持不变，每年节省四百万核时（价值NZ$320,000）。

Conclusion: 代理AI辅助的系统性重构方法能够有效解决Python科研代码的性能瓶颈问题，在保证正确性的前提下实现显著的速度提升和可观的计算资源节约。该方法为缺乏底层优化专业知识的科研人员提供了一种可行的替代方案。

Abstract: Python is widely used in scientific research because it enables rapid development and provides rich ecosystems for data analysis, artificial intelligence (AI), and machine learning. However, customized research code can become prohibitively slow as experiments scale. This challenge is particularly acute in discrete-event project-scheduling simulations, where sequential state updates, nested loops, conditional evaluations, and object-oriented structures limit the benefits of compiled numerical and GPU-accelerated libraries. Addressing these bottlenecks typically requires iterative profiling, refactoring, testing, and validation, yet researchers may lack the time or specialized software-engineering expertise for low-level optimization. This paper presents a systematic refactoring approach using Claude agentic AI on real-world project-scheduling workloads in a high-performance computing (HPC) environment. Guided by representative benchmarks and correctness checks, the agent identifies bottlenecks, implements targeted optimizations, and evaluates their effects, while the researcher retains final control. Testing runtime reduced from 1,298 seconds to under 200 seconds without changing outputs, saving four million core-hours (NZ\$320,000) annually.

</details>


### [203] [RD-Gen: Random DAG Generator Considering Multi-rate Applications for Reproducible Scheduling Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19460)
*Atsushi Yano,Takuya Azumi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出RD-Gen，一个考虑多速率应用的随机DAG生成器，用于可复现的实时系统调度评估，支持批量生成不同参数的随机DAG集，并通过案例研究验证其能管理多种问题设置和DAG研究需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时系统日益复杂，DAG模型被广泛用于表达任务依赖和并行性，但现有随机DAG生成工具无法考虑最新的多速率应用需求，研究者需自行生成DAG集，导致额外工作量且降低可靠性和可复现性。

Method: 提出RD-Gen随机DAG生成器，专门考虑多速率应用场景，支持批量生成具有不同参数的随机DAG集，以满足可复现的调度评估需求。

Result: 通过案例研究证明RD-Gen能够管理各种问题设置和DAG研究需求，验证了其有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: RD-Gen解决了现有随机DAG生成工具缺乏多速率应用支持的问题，为实时系统调度研究提供了更可靠、可复现的随机DAG生成方案。

Abstract: Real-time systems have various requirements such as the deadline and resource constraints. In addition, real-time systems are becoming larger and more complex, and studies on performance analysis and efficient scheduling algorithms are becoming increasingly important. Directed acyclic graph (DAG) models, which can express task dependencies and parallelism, are used for such studies. Random DAG sets are used to demonstrate the effectiveness and objectivity of methods proposed for real-time systems. However, there is no random DAG generation tool available that can generate a DAG set that considers the latest multi-rate applications. Therefore, researchers need to generate random DAG sets on their own, leading to additional effort and reduced reliability and reproducibility. To solve this problem, we propose a random DAG generator considering multi-rate applications for reproducible scheduling evaluation (RD-Gen). RD-Gen also enables batch generation of random DAG sets with different parameters. Case studies are used to demonstrate that RD-Gen can manage various problem settings and DAG study requirements.

</details>


### [204] [Measuring What a Specification Determines: A Formal Semantic-Block Model and an Execution-Judged Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19475)
*Oleg Grynets,Dmytro Kostetskyi,Vasyl Lyashkevych*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种形式化的语义块规范模型和基于执行评判的基准测试，用于独立于模型能力评估规范质量。模型包含语义块、依赖关系、块属规则、决策点和开放问题五个层面，并满足四个机器可检查的良构条件。在Oracle到PostgreSQL迁移规范上实例化（18个块、19条依赖边），计算验证显示五层分解将每任务上下文平均减少约71%，覆盖85.5%的Oracle构造分类体系。基准测试采用固定实现者小组、必做无规范对照组，以PostgreSQL 16和在线Oracle实例为确定性执行评判。六项设计研究（三项预注册操纵和三项诊断分析）表明：确定性作为形式概念成立，但作为独立的经验质量指标在当前LLM实现者上不成立，经验变异性下限的中位臂差离散度为14.4个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规范质量评估往往与模型能力纠缠不清，无法独立衡量规范本身的好坏。作者希望构建一个不依赖具体实现者能力的规范质量评估框架，通过形式化语义块模型和执行评判基准将规范质量与模型能力解耦。

Method: (1) 提出形式化语义块规范模型：规范由语义块、依赖关系、块属规则、决策点和开放问题构成，满足无环性、单一归属、约束支配、全定性或歧义停止四个良构条件；(2) 定义确定性的模型论概念（所有符合规范的实现之间的一致性），并通过独立实现者的收敛性进行经验估计；(3) 构建执行评判基准：固定实现者小组，设置必做无规范对照组，使用PostgreSQL 16和在线Oracle实例作为确定性执行评判；(4) 设计六项研究（三项预注册操纵、三项诊断分析）进行验证。

Result: 计算验证表明：五层分解使每任务上下文的平均依赖闭包减少约71%；覆盖Oracle构造分类体系85.5%且所有遗漏均已分诊；未被测试的替代分区帕累托支配；可从引用派生边在99.9分位恢复原结构。基准测试重复运行25单元子样本，发现中位臂差离散度为14.4个百分点的经验变异性下限。实验结果支持确定性作为形式概念，但不支持将其作为独立经验质量指标用于当前当代LLM实现者。

Conclusion: 确定性在模型论意义上是一个有效的形式概念，但作为独立于模型能力的实证质量指标，在当前LLM实现者评估中并不充分。规范质量评估仍需结合能力相关因素，该模型和基准为此提供了方法论基础。

Abstract: This work introduces a formal semantic-block model for specifications and an execution-judged benchmark for evaluating specification quality independently of model capability. A specification is represented as a structure comprising semantic blocks, dependency relations, block-owned rules, decision points, and explicitly open questions, subject to four machine-checkable well-formedness conditions: acyclicity, single ownership, constraint domination, and totality or ambiguity-stop. Determinacy is defined model-theoretically as agreement among all conforming implementations and is estimated empirically through convergence across independent implementers. The model is instantiated on an Oracle-to-PostgreSQL migration specification containing 18 blocks and 19 dependency edges. Computational validation shows that the five-layer decomposition reduces mean per-task context by approximately 71% through dependency closures, covers 85.5% of the study-defined Oracle construct taxonomy with all identified gaps triaged, is not Pareto-dominated by the tested alternative partitions, and is recovered at the 99.9th percentile from citation-derived edges not used to define the original structure. The benchmark keeps the implementer panel fixed, includes a mandatory no-specification control arm, and uses PostgreSQL 16 and a live Oracle instance as deterministic execution judges. Six designed studies, including three pre-registered manipulations and three diagnostic analyses, further examine specification effects. Repeated runs on a 25-unit subsample reveal an empirical variability floor with a median arm-delta spread of 14.4 percentage points. The results support determinacy as a formal concept but not as a standalone empirical quality metric for the evaluated contemporary LLM implementers.

</details>


### [205] [An Agentic RAG and Evaluation Framework for Assurance Case Generation: Industrial Use Case for the EU Cyber Resilience Act Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19509)
*Fariz Ikhwantri,Iker Lasa Ojanguren,Dusica Marijan,Maria I. Maslioukova,José Arias Marin,Pavlos Kosmides*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于智能体检索增强生成（agentic RAG）的自动化框架，用于生成符合欧盟网络弹性法案（CRA）要求的保证案例（ACs），并通过形式化的Claim-Argument-Evidence逻辑加以支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中小企业（SMEs）遵守欧盟CRA法规需要大量资源，因为网络安全合规性评估非常复杂。然而，这又是证明监管合规性和确保产品安全与韧性的关键环节，因此需要自动化解决方案来减轻负担。

Method: 采用基于形式化Claim-Argument-Evidence逻辑的智能体检索增强生成（agentic RAG）框架，系统性地映射技术文档要求，从而流线化生成认证证据。并设计了基于自然语言推理（NLI）的评估器来验证证据的grounding和可追溯性。

Result: 在Catalink的PATROLIoT野火监测系统案例研究中，框架生成了70个ACs，具有高grounding密度（每个AC约4.4个工件）；NLI评估器达到0.88的准确率，专家验证的可信度评分为3.06。

Conclusion: 该工作为从业者提供了一种可扩展、可解释的自动化CRA合规评估方法，在减少手动工作量的同时保持透明的决策支持，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Complying with the EU Cyber Resilience Act (CRA) is a resource-intensive challenge for SMEs due to the complexity of cybersecurity conformity assessment. Yet, it is essential for demonstrating regulatory compliance and ensuring product security and resilience. To address this, we introduce an automated framework for generating Assurance Cases (ACs) using an agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation grounded in a formal Claim-Argument-Evidence logic. By systematically mapping technical documentation requirements, the framework streamlines the generation of certification evidence. We validate our approach on a case study of Catalink's PATROLIoT wildfire monitoring system, where the agentic RAG generated 70 ACs with high grounding density (~4.4 artefacts per AC). The proposed Natural Language Inference (NLI) evaluator achieves 0.88 accuracy, which provides robust evidence grounding and traceability, while expert-validated plausibility (3.06) supports interpretable justifications. For practitioners, this work provides a scalable, interpretable approach for automating mandatory CRA conformity assessments, reducing manual effort while maintaining transparent decision support.

</details>


### [206] [Trace-Based Execution-Level Observability of VDM-SL Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19510)
*Tomohiro Oda,Han-Myung Chang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出在VDM（Vienna Development Method）中记录和执行操作内部行为的执行轨迹（包括赋值、操作调用和返回语句），并将其作为基于状态的模型进行持久化和分析，同时在ViennaTalk中实现该数据模型并应用于可视化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VDM虽然支持严格的数学定理证明和模拟执行的软件测试，但现有的动画和调试器执行方式无法持久化记录操作内部行为，难以对执行过程中的内部状态进行深入分析和可视化，因此需要一种将执行轨迹记录并作为状态模型进行分析的方法。

Method: 提出记录赋值、操作调用和返回语句三类事件的执行轨迹数据模型；在ViennaTalk工具中实现该数据模型；将该模型应用于可视化，使操作的内部行为可持久化和可分析。

Result: 在ViennaTalk中成功实现了执行轨迹的数据模型，并展示了其在可视化方面的应用，使操作的内部行为能够以基于状态模型的形式进行持久化分析和展示。

Conclusion: 通过记录和利用执行轨迹，VDM规范的内部操作行为可以被持久保存并作为状态模型进行分析和可视化，从而增强规范验证和调试的能力。

Abstract: VDM has been pursuing rigorous verification through mathematical theorem proving and software testing via simulated execution. Animation through an interpreter enables validation of the specification to ensure it meets the required functionality. Step-by-step execution in a debugger also allows the user to follow the internal behavior of operations. In this paper, we propose the recording and utilization of execution traces of assignments, operation calls, and return statements to make the internal behavior of operations persistent and analyzable as state-based models. The data model of events in execution traces, its implementation in ViennaTalk, and its application to visualization will be introduced.

</details>


### [207] [Garbage Collection and Energy Consumption in Java: A Controlled Study Across Workloads and JDKs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19520)
*Rahil Sharma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过受控实验评估了Serial、Parallel和G1三种垃圾收集器在三个Java应用、三种工作负载强度和两个JDK发行版（OpenJDK和Oracle JDK）下的能耗表现。结果显示Parallel能耗最低（839.8J），但ANOVA分析未证明收集器之间存在统计显著的能耗差异；相反，工作负载强度才是能耗的主要驱动因素。研究结论认为不存在普适的能源最优垃圾收集器，收集器选择应作为基于目标系统测量数据的应用特定调优决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 垃圾收集器选择是一个低成本的配置决策，能影响Java应用的性能和能耗，但现有的能耗排名是否能在异构应用、工作负载强度和JDK发行版之间泛化仍不明确。作者旨在通过受控实验回答这一开放问题，避免依赖可能误导的聚合排名。

Method: 采用受控实证评估方法：在三个Java应用、三种工作负载强度和两个JDK发行版（OpenJDK和Oracle JDK）的组合下，对Serial、Parallel和G1三种垃圾收集器进行测试。使用EnergiBridge工具测量处理器封装能耗和执行时间，并导出互补的能耗-性能指标，最后用RCBD ANOVA进行统计分析。

Result: Parallel平均能耗最低（839.8J），Serial次之（857.6J），G1最高（969.0J），但RCBD ANOVA未建立统计上可靠的收集器效应。工作负载强度是能耗的显著驱动因素，重负载消耗显著更多能量。能耗与执行时间呈中等正相关（r=0.33）。OpenJDK与Oracle JDK之间无统计显著的能耗差异。

Conclusion: 研究不支持存在普遍能源最优的垃圾收集器。工作负载强度而非收集器选择，是管理Java能耗更可靠的杠杆。收集器选择应作为应用特定的调优决策，并需基于目标系统上的实际测量，而不是依赖聚合排名。

Abstract: Garbage-collector selection is a low-effort configuration decision that can influence both the performance and energy consumption of Java applications. However, it remains unclear whether aggregate energy rankings among collectors generalise across heterogeneous applications, workload intensities, and JDK distributions. This study presents a controlled empirical evaluation of Serial, Parallel, and G1 garbage collection across three Java applications, three workload intensities, and two JDK distributions: OpenJDK and Oracle JDK. Using EnergiBridge, processor-package energy consumption and execution time were measured and complementary energy-performance metrics derived. Across all evaluated configurations, Parallel recorded the lowest mean energy consumption (839.8 J), followed closely by Serial (857.6 J) and G1 (969.0 J), but an RCBD ANOVA did not establish a statistically reliable collector effect. Workload intensity, by contrast, was a significant driver of energy consumption, with heavy workloads consuming substantially more energy than light or medium workloads regardless of collector. Energy consumption showed a moderate positive association with execution time (r = 0.33), indicating that longer-running configurations tended toward higher energy use, though the relationship was far from proportional. No statistically significant energy difference was found between OpenJDK and Oracle JDK. Overall, the results do not support a universally energy-optimal garbage collector; instead, workload intensity emerges as the more reliable lever for managing Java energy consumption, and collector selection should be treated as an application-specific tuning decision supported by measurement on the target system rather than aggregate rankings alone.

</details>


### [208] [Auditing and Decomposing Feedback-Driven Evolution in LLM Test Generation under the Oracle Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19626)
*Yunhao Liang,Chengguang Gan,Ruixuan Ying,Hanjun Wei,Zhe Cui,Shiwen Ni*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文审计了反馈驱动测试生成中"单一参考程序执行反馈"作为自验证信号的可靠性问题。研究发现，当生成输入在单一被接受程序上执行并将其输出作为ground truth时，无效或欠规格输入会产生虚假的故障检测和表观进化收益。通过142个开发任务、114个锁定外部任务和138个保留任务的系统审计，发现单一参考oracle将进化收益夸大了9.46-14.85个百分点，而外部输入中仅27.79%和50.12%的生成输出与三实现一致的面板匹配。审计后，等预算独立重采样比基于突变的进化表现更好。论文提出了一种"审计+安慰剂"协议，用于分离验证器伪影、交互脚手架和真实反馈贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 执行反馈常被视为改进LLM生成测试的自验证信号，但在单一被接受程序上执行生成输入并以其输出作为ground truth时，无效或欠规格输入会导致虚假故障检测和虚假的进化收益。研究旨在审计这一失败模式，揭示反馈驱动测试生成中评价协议的缺陷。

Method: 使用142个开发任务、114个锁定外部任务和138个保留任务，结合两个代码模型、三个种子和故障交叉拟合的真实提交进行系统性审计。引入三实现一致性面板作为参照，比较单一参考oracle与面板oracle的差异。设计三种对比：单一参考oracle vs 面板oracle、基于突变的进化 vs 等预算独立重采样、真实三轮反馈循环 vs 密度匹配安慰剂。由两名软件工程博士生进行盲化语义审计。

Result: 外部输入中生成输出与三实现面板匹配率仅27.79%和50.12%；单一参考oracle将进化收益夸大9.46-14.85个百分点；审计后等预算独立重采样优于基于突变的进化6.01-18.83个百分点；真实反馈与安慰剂的差异极小（外部+0.13和-0.50点，保留+1.99和+0.28点），未提供细粒度反馈收益的稳健证据；盲化审计分类94.41%的面板不确认输入为无效，但3.60%为有效。

Conclusion: 单一参考执行反馈作为ground truth并不可靠，面板分歧具有信息量但不等同于语义确证。论文提出"审计+安慰剂"协议以分离验证器伪影、交互脚手架和真实反馈贡献，为自我进化测试生成器的评估提供更严谨的方法论框架。

Abstract: Execution feedback is often treated as a self-verifying signal for improving LLM-generated tests. However, when generated inputs are executed on a single accepted program and its outputs are used as ground truth, invalid or underspecified inputs can create spurious fault detections and apparent evolutionary gains. We audit this failure mode in feedback-driven test generation using 142 development tasks, 114 locked external tasks, and 138 held-out tasks, with two code models, three seeds, and fault-cross-fitted real submissions. On external inputs for which three accepted implementations agree, generated outputs match the panel on only 27.79% and 50.12% of cases. A single-reference oracle inflates the measured gain from evolution by 9.46-14.85 percentage points; after auditing, equal-budget independent resampling outperforms mutation-based evolution by 6.01-18.83 points. We further compare a genuine three-round feedback loop with a density-matched placebo. External Real-Placebo differences are +0.13 and -0.50 points, while held-out differences are +1.99 and +0.28 points and do not provide robust evidence of fine-grained feedback benefit. A blinded semantic audit by two software engineering doctoral students classifies 94.41% of panel-disconfirmed inputs as invalid but 3.60% as valid, showing that panel disagreement is informative but not semantic proof. We propose an audit-and-placebo protocol that separates verifier artifacts, interaction scaffolding, and grounded feedback credit in evaluations of self-evolving test generators.

</details>


### [209] [Loreley: Repository-Scale Program Evolution with Quality-Diversity Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19703)
*Mohan Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍Loreley，一种基于质量-多样性（QD）存档的顺序智能体搜索方法。与顺序冠军编辑（只保留当前最优）和独立根提议（完全重启）不同，Loreley在QD存档中保留完整的仓库状态，并从中采样作为父代或作为后续编辑的上下文。实验在Zstandard基准上进行了对照实验（1008个总任务），结果QD在48个任务时比顺序冠军低0.135%，比独立根高0.320%，但统计上均未确立优势。存档保留和后续采样确实发生了，但受控实验未显示端到端效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序智能体搜索从当前最优解累积变化但丢弃替代分支，独立提议保留广度却从根节点重启。作者希望结合两者优势：既保留探索广度，又能利用已有进展，从而克服单一策略的局限。

Method: Loreley在质量-多样性（QD）存档中保留完整的仓库状态（Git提交），从中采样作为父代或作为后续编辑的上下文。候选在隔离的worktree中生成，由项目提供的评估器评判。实验设计为匹配的Zstandard实验：7个配对块，每策略每块48个物理候选任务（共1008个），根初始化，各策略用原生并发方式运行。

Result: 在48个任务时，QD比顺序冠军低0.135%（95% BCa区间：-0.556%到+0.161%），比独立根高0.320%（95%区间：-0.082%到+0.686%）。两个对比均未确立QD优势；顺序策略在48任务时均值和单位数最高。存档保留和后续采样确实发生：7个最终QD胜者中有4个主父系祖先中包含非在位状态，加入灵感边后升至6个。

Conclusion: Loreley确实实现了预期的"垫脚石"（stepping-stone）机制——存档被保留、被采样、且非在位状态出现在父系祖先中——但受控实验未显示48任务预算下的端点收益。三个早期能力战役在Python库和Zstandard中产生了第4代多文件改进，说明机制有潜力，但需进一步研究以证明其优势。

Abstract: Sequential agent search accumulates changes from its current champion but discards alternative branches; independent proposals preserve breadth but restart from the root. Loreley instead retains complete repository states in a Quality-Diversity (QD) archive and samples them as parents or supplies them as context for later edits. Candidates are Git commits produced in isolated worktrees and judged by a project-supplied evaluator. We compare configured Loreley QD, sequential champion editing, and independent root proposals in a matched Zstandard experiment: seven paired blocks and 48 physical candidate jobs per policy and block (1,008 total), with root-only initialization and each policy's native concurrency. Validation selected a winner at each budget checkpoint; an agent-hidden holdout measured the fixed candidate. At 48 jobs, QD was 0.135% below Sequential Champion (95% BCa interval for the paired effect: -0.556% to +0.161%) and 0.320% above Independent Root (-0.082% to +0.686%). Neither contrast established a QD advantage; Sequential had the highest observed 48-job mean and median. Archive retention and later sampling did occur. Four of seven final QD winners had a non-incumbent state in their primary-parent ancestry under a retrospective one-incumbent rule applied only to the observed QD stream. Including inspiration edges raised the count to six, without showing that supplied context caused an edit. Three earlier capability campaigns produced generation-4, multi-file improvements in two Python libraries and a separate Zstandard revision. Loreley engaged the intended stepping-stone mechanism, but the controlled experiment did not show an endpoint benefit at 48 jobs.

</details>


### [210] [A Fully Automated, Deployment-Aware Testing Pipeline for IoT-Based Automotive Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19752)
*Denesa Zyberaj,Roman Vintonyak,Pascal Hirmer,Marco Aiello*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端、部署感知的汽车嵌入式软件测试流水线，结合LLM/VLM辅助的需求驱动测试与代码生成、人在回路精炼，并通过Eclipse openDuT实现分布式部署。在儿童存在检测系统（CPDS）案例中实现了全部9个需求的功能覆盖和100%的Gherkin生成准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代汽车嵌入式软件测试面临系统复杂性高、架构去中心化、安全与性能约束严格等挑战，传统手动测试方法效率低且一致性差，亟需自动化且支持分布式部署的测试方案。

Method: 提出端到端测试流水线：(1) 结合LLM和VLM进行需求驱动的测试与代码生成；(2) 引入人在回路（human-in-the-loop）进行精炼以减少手动工作并提高一致性；(3) 基于Eclipse openDuT支持跨地理位置、跨组织的分布式部署，优化节点可用性和跨组织协调。

Result: 在CPDS案例研究中：9个功能需求全部实现全覆盖（100%）；受控需求集上Gherkin生成准确率达100%；通过Eclipse openDuT在跨地理位置的ECU上成功执行分布式测试，验证了OEM-供应商测试工作流的可行性。

Conclusion: 该流水线有效结合了LLM/VLM自动化与人工监督，在保证测试质量的同时降低了手动开销，并通过分布式执行架构适配了现代汽车行业OEM与供应商协同开发的真实场景。

Abstract: Testing embedded software in modern vehicles is challenging due to system complexity, decentralized architectures, and strict safety and performance constraints. In this work, we present an end-to-end, deployment-aware testing pipeline for IoT-based automotive applications. The pipeline combines requirement-driven test and code generation with large language model (LLM) and vision-language model (VLM) assistance, and human-in-the-loop curation to reduce manual effort and improve consistency. Using Eclipse openDuT, it supports flexible, distributed deployment across geographically separated cyber-physical and IoT infrastructures, optimizing for node availability and cross-organizational coordination. For validation, we conduct a case study using a Child Presence Detection System (CPDS), achieving full functional requirement coverage across all 9 requirements and 100% Gherkin generation accuracy on the controlled requirement set. Distributed test execution across geographically separated ECUs via Eclipse openDuT confirms the pipeline's applicability to OEM--supplier testing workflows.

</details>


### [211] [PRAXIS: Graph-Grounded Tacit Knowledge for Domain Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19784)
*Xue Jiang,Tianyu Zhang,Lingwei Wu,Ziyu Wang,Ge Li,Yuan Sui,Hao Zhu,Wenpin Jiao,Zhi Jin,Yihong Dong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出PRAXIS框架，通过模拟人类开发流程、将隐性知识蒸馏为结构化单元并组织在代码依赖图上，在代码交互点主动呈现给agent，从而解决LLM agent在领域特定代码生成中因缺乏隐性知识（业务规则、接口契约、操作约定）而表现不佳的问题。实验表明PRAXIS优于现有最先进的agent方法，且可无缝集成到不同agent框架和LLM中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent在通用软件工程任务上表现出色，但在领域特定代码生成上表现不佳。根因是agent缺乏开发者通过实践内化但从未文档化的隐性知识（tacit knowledge），包括领域业务规则、接口契约和操作约定。这些知识深埋于领域代码中，分散在代码实体及其依赖关系中，对缺乏该知识的agent不可见，使其难以检索或学习。

Method: PRAXIS框架包含三个核心环节：(1) 获取——通过模拟目标代码库中的人类开发工作流来提取隐性知识；(2) 表示——将知识蒸馏为结构化单元，并组织在代码依赖图上；(3) 重用——在代码交互的关键时刻主动将相关知识呈递给agent，实现前瞻性的知识供给。

Result: 广泛实验表明，PRAXIS优于配备强大agentic搜索能力的SOTA agent，以及基于经验和基于技能的方法。该框架能无缝集成到各种agent框架和LLM中并带来一致的性能提升，且支持持续演化——随着实践积累，性能稳步提升。

Conclusion: PRAXIS通过系统性地提取、表示和重用隐性知识，有效解决了LLM agent在领域代码生成中的关键瓶颈。其与不同agent框架和LLM的兼容性以及随实践积累持续进化的能力，为领域特定代码生成提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: LLM agents have achieved strong performance on general software engineering tasks, yet struggle with domain-specific code generation. We identify the root cause as the agent's lack of tacit knowledge, including domain-specific business rules, interface contracts, and operational conventions that developers internalize through practice but never document. This knowledge is deeply buried beneath the domain code, dispersed across code entities and their dependency relations, and invisible to the agent that lacks it. These properties make tacit knowledge inherently difficult to retrieve or learn. In this work, we propose PRAXIS, a framework that enables agents to systematically extract, represent, and reuse tacit knowledge for domain code generation. PRAXIS acquires tacit knowledge by simulating human development workflows within the target codebase, distills it into structured units organized on the code dependency graph, and proactively surfaces it to the agent at the point of code interaction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PRAXIS outperforms state-of-the-art agents equipped with powerful agentic search capabilities, as well as experience-based and skill-based methods. The approach integrates seamlessly into various agent frameworks and LLMs with consistent performance improvements, and supports continual evolution with performance steadily scaling as practice accumulates.

</details>


### [212] [Further Progress Towards Operation Proof Obligation Generation for VDM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19848)
*Nick Battle,Carlo Rende,Peter Gorm Larsen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是对VDM形式化方法中证明义务生成工作的更新，改进了循环不变量、循环变体、操作调用的证明义务（包括隐式声明操作和规范语句），并为操作添加了递归度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VDM形式化方法通过证明义务来确保模型内部一致性，但原有的证明义务生成方法在处理循环不变式、操作调用和递归操作等方面存在不足，需要改进以提升验证能力。

Method: 本文改进了循环不变式的处理方法，新增了循环变体的支持，完善了操作调用（含隐式声明操作和规范语句）的证明义务生成，并为操作引入了递归度量。

Result: 通过示例展示了各项新功能的实际应用，验证了改进后的证明义务生成方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 更新后的证明义务生成方法更全面地覆盖了VDM操作验证中的关键场景，进一步增强了VDM形式化方法的内部一致性保证能力。

Abstract: The VDM formalism can ensure that models are internally consistent. Potential inconsistencies are highlighted by assertions called proof obligations. This paper is an update to the work described in [1], regarding proof obligation generation for VDM operations. We present the latest work which improves the approach to loop invariants, adds loop variants, improves proof obligations for operation calls, including implicitly declared operations and specification statements, and adds recursive measures for operations. The new features are illustrated with examples.

</details>


### [213] [Repo0: Design-Driven Zero-to-All Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19854)
*Silin Chen,Haoyi Teng,Xiaodong Gu,Yuling Shi,Jiale Huang,Yongpan Wang,Hongyu Zhang,Haibing Guan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Repo0是一个面向"零到全量"代码生成的连续结构演化框架，通过维护一个由需求级DAG、组件级DAG及其对齐关系组成的双有向无环图（Dual-DAG）作为显式架构状态，迭代地通过模块化度量引导的结构化操作演化组件边界，直到结构收敛后再进行测试驱动的代码生成。在六个真实仓库上，Repo0相比最强的仓库规划基线RPG在功能覆盖率和通过率上分别最多提升20.08和29.74个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型智能体在代码生成方面虽有进展，但大多数系统假设预定义的仓库架构。这种假设在"零到全量"代码生成场景中不成立——该场景要求智能体直接从自然语言需求构建整个软件项目，并在整个开发过程中保持模块化的仓库架构。

Method: Repo0维护一个显式的架构状态（Dual-DAG），包含需求级DAG、组件级DAG以及两者的对齐关系。从自然语言需求出发，它通过由模块化指标引导的结构化操作迭代演化组件边界，直至结构收敛，随后用收敛后的架构指导测试驱动的代码生成。

Result: 在来自RepoCraft的六个真实仓库上，使用GPT-5 mini和DeepSeek V3.2评估，Repo0在所有设置中取得了最高的功能覆盖率和通过率。相比最强的仓库规划基线RPG，功能覆盖率最多提升20.08个百分点，通过率最多提升29.74个百分点。消融实验和结构演化分析进一步验证了Dual-DAG架构状态、模块化引导的结构演化以及显式结构收敛的重要性。

Conclusion: 通过显式维护架构状态并持续演化结构，Repo0在零到全量代码生成任务中显著优于现有方法，证明了架构演化与结构收敛对长周期软件项目构建的关键价值。

Abstract: Large language model agents have made substantial progress in code generation, yet most existing systems assume a predefined repository architecture. This assumption does not hold in zero-to-all code generation, where an agent must construct an entire software project directly from natural-language requirements while maintaining a modular repository architecture throughout development. We present Repo0, a continuous structural evolution framework for zero-to-all code generation. Repo0 maintains an explicit architectural state instantiated as a Dual-Directed-Acyclic-Graph (Dual-DAG), consisting of a requirement-level DAG, a component-level DAG, and their alignment relation. Starting from natural-language requirements, it iteratively evolves component boundaries through structural actions guided by modularity metrics until structural convergence, after which the converged architecture guides test-driven development code generation. We evaluate Repo0 on six real-world repositories from RepoCraft using GPT-5 mini and DeepSeek V3.2. Repo0 achieves the highest Functionality Coverage and Pass Rate across all settings. Compared with RPG, the strongest repository-planning baseline, Repo0 improves Functionality Coverage by up to 20.08 percentage points and Pass Rate by up to 29.74 percentage points. Ablation and structural-evolution analyses further demonstrate the importance of the Dual-DAG architectural state, modularity-guided structural evolution, and explicit structural convergence.

</details>


### [214] [Evaluating Automated Testing on an Open-Source Web Application Using Cypress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19960)
*Quoc-Binh Nguyen,Truc-Ly Phan Nguyen,Ngoc Hong Tran,Dung Hai Dinh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了基于Cypress框架的端到端自动化测试在开源Web应用中的有效性，通过27个测试用例从执行速度、可靠性和可维护性三个维度进行衡量。结果表明Cypress测试套件执行时间短且稳定，适合频繁回归测试；大部分测试结果一致，仅有涉及复杂交互的少数测试出现不稳定；同时发现数据属性定位策略和Page Object Model架构能显著降低UI变更时的维护成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动化测试在Web开发中广泛应用，但其实际效果受执行时间、结果稳定性和测试套件可维护性等多重因素影响。作者希望通过实证研究系统评估Cypress框架在这些关键维度上的表现，为开发团队选择和使用端到端测试框架提供依据。

Method: 选取开源Web应用作为实验对象，使用Cypress框架设计并部署27个端到端测试用例，从执行速度、可靠性（多次运行结果一致性）和可维护性（元素定位策略与POM架构的影响）三个维度进行实验评估。

Result: 实验结果显示：Cypress测试套件执行时间短且稳定，适合软件开发过程中的频繁运行；大多数测试用例在多次运行中结果一致，仅有涉及复杂交互功能的少数测试出现flaky现象；弹性强的data-cy属性定位策略结合POM架构可显著降低UI变化时的维护开销。

Conclusion: Cypress框架在端到端自动化测试中表现出良好的执行效率和可靠性，是Web软件持续开发中可行的测试方案；通过合理的元素定位策略（如data-cy属性）和POM架构设计，可有效提升测试套件的可维护性，减少UI演进带来的维护负担。

Abstract: End-to-end automated testing is increasingly used in web software development to ensure system quality and shorten response times during development. However, the true effectiveness of automated testing depends on many factors including execution time, stability of test results, and maintainability of the test suite as the application continues to evolve. In this paper, we evaluate the effectiveness of end-to-end automated testing using the Cypress framework for an open-source web application. We deployed the experiment with 27 test cases. The test's effectiveness is measured by execution speed, reliability, and maintainability. The experimental results show that the Cypress-based end-to-end test suite has short and stable execution times. It suits frequent runs during software development. The majority of test cases achieved consistent results across multiple runs, while flakiness only occurred in a few tests which involve complex interactive functions. Furthermore, the study highlights the impact of element locator strategies and Page Object Model (POM) architecture on test suite maintainability, demonstrating that resilient data-cy attributes significantly reduce maintenance overhead when UI changes occur.

</details>


### [215] [BreakGuard: Towards Detecting Dependency Breaking Changes with LLM-Generated Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20167)
*Rachna Raj,Benoit Baudry,Diego Elias Costa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: BreakGuard是一种利用LLM生成测试套件来检测客户端代码中库破坏性变更（BCs）的方法，通过在89个真实BCs上评估，使用最佳配置可检测30.3%的BCs，平均每个检测到的BC成本约0.90美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源库在演进过程中引入的破坏性变更（BCs）会破坏客户端应用的运行时行为，而客户端现有的测试套件由于库覆盖率有限，往往无法检测到这些BCs。

Method: BreakGuard静态提取每个调用目标库方法的客户端焦点方法，然后为每个焦点方法生成测试。测试在库的破坏前版本上通过但在破坏版本上失败，则判定检测到BC。使用3种LLM（GPT4o、Qwen3-coder-480B、GPT-OSS-120B）和三种上下文级别（最小、方法、类）进行评估。

Result: 在BUMP数据集的89个真实破坏性变更上，使用最佳配置检测到30.3%（27/89）的BCs，平均每个检测到的BC成本约0.90美元。成功检测到不同库类别的BCs（如JSON库、日志、解析），但发现LLM生成的测试对崩溃型BCs更可靠，而非行为型BCs。

Conclusion: BreakGuard展示了利用LLM生成测试来检测库破坏性变更的可行性，但当前方法对行为型BCs的检测能力有限，需要进一步改进以提升整体检测率。

Abstract: Open-source libraries play an important role in software development by providing reusable features that expedite the development process. As libraries evolve, they release new versions that add features, fix bugs, or apply security patches. In this process, they may break the contract established with their clients by introducing breaking changes (BCs) that alter the runtime behavior and break client applications. Client-side test suites often fail to detect these BCs because of limited library coverage that does not exercise all library methods used in the client's codebase.
  We propose BreakGuard, an approach that generates a test suite to detect breaking changes in clients. BreakGuard statically extracts every client method (focal method) that invokes the target library method (call site), then generates tests per focal method. A test detects a BC if it passes on the pre-breaking version and fails on the breaking version. We evaluate our approach on 89 real-world breaking changes from the BUMP dataset, using 3 LLMs (GPT4o, Qwen3-coder-480B, GPT-OSS-120B) and three context levels: minimal, method, and class. Using the best-performing configuration, BreakGuard detects 30.3% of breaking changes (27 of 89) at a mean cost of roughly $0.90 USD per detected breaking change. We successfully detected BCs from different library categories (e.g., JSON libraries, logging, parsing), but we find LLM-generated tests to be more reliable for detecting crash-type breaking changes as opposed to behavioural BCs.

</details>


### [216] [From Agent Behaviour to Agent-Friendly Documentation: An Empirical Study of How Coding Agents Discover, Read, and Write Technical Documentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20195)
*Zhijun Gao,Jing Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过对两个公开数据集（557个SWE-chat会话和33,097个AIDev拉取请求）的行为级研究，揭示了AI编码智能体与文档交互的真实模式。研究发现：智能体的文档工作主要围绕智能体面向的工件（指令文件和工作笔记占60.5%），而非传统技术文档；文档咨询与代码编辑的关联在调整后模型中显著（OR 1.33），但缺乏明确的基于文档的验证序列；咨询与即时测试呈负相关；文档交互多为自主发起（70.2%）而非失败驱动（7.5%）。作者提出一个"双叶循环"描述性模型，并挑战了"智能体友好"文档的两个核心假设（可行动性和可验证性）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主编码智能体在软件开发中扮演越来越重要的角色，技术文档的消费模式发生了根本性变化。然而，目前对智能体如何查阅文档、何时查阅以及查阅后产生什么影响缺乏系统的行为层面理解，这严重制约了文档实践的发展。

Method: 采用行为扎根研究方法，分析两个公开数据集：SWE-chat（557个智能体编码会话，94,813个开发事件，含3,033次文档交互）和AIDev（33,097个智能体拉取请求，690,260条文件级变更记录）。使用事件级分析、邻接转移概率、提升度和阶段调整模型（OR）等方法量化文档交互模式。

Result: 四个主要发现：（1）文档工作主要由智能体面向工件主导（60.5%），传统技术文档仅占10.6%；（2）文档咨询与代码编辑的关联需阶段调整后才显著（OR 1.33 [1.09, 1.62]）；（3）未观察到显式基于文档的验证序列，咨询与即时测试呈负相关（OR 0.39）；(4）文档咨询多为自主发起（70.2%）而非失败驱动（7.5%），且代码先于文档被修改（4.7倍）。
